```
    46→  # so a relative path works. In local dev (selfsigned TLS), Rails may be

    48→  def terminal_redirect_url(path)

    53→  public

    55→  # Called by Traefik forwardAuth. Returns 200 to allow, or redirects to
    56→  # login (which Traefik passes through to the browser).
    57→  def auth
    58→    forwarded_uri = request.headers["X-Forwarded-Uri"] || ""
    59→    match = forwarded_uri.match(%r{/terminal/(\d+)/wetty})
    60→    head(:unauthorized) and return unless match
    61→
    62→    session_record = find_session_by_cookie
    63→    unless session_record
    64→      # Build the original terminal URL from Traefik's forwarded headers
    65→      # so the user returns here after logging in.
    66→      proto = request.headers["X-Forwarded-Proto"] || "https"
    67→      host  = request.headers["X-Forwarded-Host"] || request.host_with_port
    68→      original_url = "#{proto}://#{host}#{forwarded_uri}"
    69→      session[:return_to_after_authenticating] = original_url
    70→      redirect_to new_session_url, allow_other_host: true
    71→      return
    72→    end
    73→
    74→    user = session_record.user
    75→    sandbox = Sandbox.active.find_by(id: match[1].to_i)
    76→    head(:unauthorized) and return unless sandbox && (sandbox.user_id == user.id || user.admin?)
    77→
    78→    head :ok
    79→  end
    80→end
```

```
     1→class TerminalManager
     2→  DATA_DIR = ENV.fetch("SANDCASTLE_DATA_DIR", "/data")
     3→  WETTY_IMAGE = ENV.fetch("SANDCASTLE_WETTY_IMAGE", "wettyoss/wetty:latest")

     7→  class Error < StandardError; end
     8→
     9→  # Opens a web terminal for the given sandbox.
    10→  # Returns the URL path to the WeTTY session.
    11→  def open(sandbox:)
    12→    raise Error, "Sandbox is not running" unless sandbox.status == "running"
    13→    raise Error, "Sandbox has no container" if sandbox.container_id.blank?
    14→
    15→    user = sandbox.user
    16→    container_name = wetty_container_name(sandbox)
    17→
    18→    # Idempotent: if WeTTY container already running, return URL
    19→    if container_running?(container_name)
    20→      return wetty_url(sandbox)
    21→    end
    22→
    23→    pull_image
    24→    ensure_network
    25→    connect_sandbox_to_network(sandbox)
    26→
    27→    # Write Traefik config early so it has time to detect the new route
    28→    # while we set up keypairs and start the WeTTY container.
    29→    write_traefik_config(sandbox)
    30→
    31→    key_dir = generate_keypair(sandbox)
    32→    inject_pubkey(sandbox, key_dir)
    33→    create_wetty_container(sandbox: sandbox, user: user, key_dir: key_dir)
    34→
    35→    # Give Traefik time to detect the new route configuration
    36→    # before the wait page starts polling. Traefik's file watcher
    37→    # typically picks up changes within 1-2 seconds.
    38→    sleep 2
    39→
    40→    wetty_url(sandbox)
    41→  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError => e
    42→    raise Error, "Failed to open terminal: #{e.message}"
    43→  rescue SystemCallError => e
    44→    raise Error, "Failed to open terminal: #{e.message}"
    45→  end
    46→
    47→  # Closes the web terminal for the given sandbox.
    48→  def close(sandbox:)
    49→    container_name = wetty_container_name(sandbox)
    50→
    51→    # Stop and remove WeTTY container
    52→    begin
    53→      container = Docker::Container.get(container_name)
    54→      container.stop(t: 3) rescue nil
    55→      container.delete(force: true)
    56→    rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
    57→      # Already gone
    58→    end
    59→
    60→    # Delete Traefik config
    61→    delete_traefik_config(sandbox)
    62→
    63→    # Clean up key directory
    64→    key_dir = key_dir_path(sandbox)
    65→    FileUtils.rm_rf(key_dir)
    66→
    67→    # Best-effort: remove pubkey from sandbox authorized_keys
    68→    remove_pubkey(sandbox)
    69→  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError => e
    70→    Rails.logger.error("TerminalManager: close failed for #{sandbox.full_name}: #{e.message}")
    71→  end
    72→
    73→  # Returns true if the WeTTY container is running for this sandbox.
    74→  def active?(sandbox:)

    77→
    78→  # Removes orphaned WeTTY containers whose sandbox no longer exists or is not running.
    79→  def cleanup_orphaned
    80→    Docker::Container.all(all: true).each do |container|
    81→      name = container.info.dig("Names")&.first&.delete_prefix("/")
    82→      next unless name&.start_with?("sc-wetty-")
    83→
    84→      labels = container.info["Labels"] || {}
    85→      sandbox_id = labels["sandcastle.sandbox_id"]&.to_i
    86→
    87→      sandbox = sandbox_id ? Sandbox.find_by(id: sandbox_id) : nil
    88→      should_remove = sandbox.nil? || sandbox.status != "running"
    89→
    90→      if should_remove
    91→        container.stop(t: 3) rescue nil
    92→        container.delete(force: true)
    93→        Rails.logger.info("TerminalManager: removed orphaned WeTTY container #{name}")
    94→
    95→        # Clean up Traefik config and keys if we have a sandbox_id
    96→        if sandbox_id
    97→          config_path = File.join(DYNAMIC_DIR, "terminal-#{sandbox_id}.yml")
    98→          File.delete(config_path) if File.exist?(config_path)
    99→        end
   100→
   101→        # Extract full_name from container name and validate format
   102→        full_name = name.delete_prefix("sc-wetty-")
   103→        unless full_name.match?(/\A[a-z][a-z0-9_-]+-[a-z][a-z0-9_-]*\z/)
   104→          Rails.logger.warn("TerminalManager: skipping suspicious container name: #{name}")
   105→          next
   106→        end
   107→
   108→        key_dir = File.join(DATA_DIR, "wetty", full_name)
   109→        expected_parent = File.join(DATA_DIR, "wetty")
   110→        unless File.expand_path(key_dir).start_with?("#{File.expand_path(expected_parent)}/")
   111→          Rails.logger.warn("TerminalManager: path traversal attempt detected: #{key_dir}")
   112→          next
   113→        end
   114→
   115→        FileUtils.rm_rf(key_dir) if Dir.exist?(key_dir)
   116→      end
   117→    end
   118→  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError => e
   119→    Rails.logger.error("TerminalManager: orphan cleanup failed: #{e.message}")
   120→  end
   121→
   122→  private
   123→
   124→  def wetty_container_name(sandbox)
   125→    "sc-wetty-#{sandbox.full_name}"
   126→  end
   127→
   128→  def wetty_url(sandbox)
   129→    "/terminal/#{sandbox.id}/wetty"
   130→  end
   131→
   132→  def key_dir_path(sandbox)
   133→    File.join(DATA_DIR, "wetty", sandbox.full_name)
   134→  end
   135→
   136→  def container_running?(name)
   137→    container = Docker::Container.get(name)
   138→    container.json.dig("State", "Running") == true
   139→  rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
   140→    false
   141→  end
   142→
   143→  def pull_image
   144→    Docker::Image.get(WETTY_IMAGE)
   145→  rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
   146→    Docker::Image.create("fromImage" => WETTY_IMAGE)
   147→  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError
   148→    raise Error, "Failed to pull #{WETTY_IMAGE} — check network connectivity"
   149→  end
   150→
   151→  def ensure_network
   152→    Docker::Network.get(NETWORK_NAME)
   153→  rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
   154→    Docker::Network.create(NETWORK_NAME, "Driver" => "bridge")
   155→  end
   156→
   157→  def connect_sandbox_to_network(sandbox)
   158→    return unless sandbox.container_id.present?
   159→
   160→    network = Docker::Network.get(NETWORK_NAME)
   161→    container = Docker::Container.get(sandbox.container_id)
   162→
   163→    networks = container.json.dig("NetworkSettings", "Networks") || {}
   164→    return if networks.key?(NETWORK_NAME)
   165→
   166→    network.connect(sandbox.container_id)
   167→  rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
   168→    # Container no longer exists - sync job will fix the DB state
   169→    raise Error, "Sandbox container not found. Please refresh and try again."
   170→  end
   171→
   172→  def generate_keypair(sandbox)
   173→    key_dir = key_dir_path(sandbox)
   174→    FileUtils.mkdir_p(key_dir, mode: 0o700)
   175→
   176→    key_path = File.join(key_dir, "key")
   177→    # Remove old keys if they exist
   178→    FileUtils.rm_f(key_path)
   179→    FileUtils.rm_f("#{key_path}.pub")
   180→
   181→    system("ssh-keygen", "-t", "ed25519", "-f", key_path, "-N", "", "-q", "-C", "wetty-#{sandbox.full_name}",
   182→      exception: true)
   183→
   184→    # Ensure private key is only readable by owner
   185→    File.chmod(0o600, key_path)
   186→
   187→    key_dir
   188→  end
   189→
   190→  def inject_pubkey(sandbox, key_dir)
   191→    pubkey = File.read(File.join(key_dir, "key.pub")).strip
   192→    username = sandbox.user.name
   193→
   194→    container = Docker::Container.get(sandbox.container_id)
   195→
   196→    # Write pubkey via base64 to avoid any shell-injection risk.
   197→    # The stdin: approach hangs because docker-api never sends EOF to cat.
   198→    encoded = Base64.strict_encode64("#{pubkey}\n")
   199→    container.exec([ "mkdir", "-p", "/home/#{username}/.ssh" ])
   200→    container.exec([ "sh", "-c", "echo #{encoded} | base64 -d >> /home/#{username}/.ssh/authorized_keys" ])
   201→    container.exec([ "chown", "-R", "#{username}:#{username}", "/home/#{username}/.ssh" ])
   202→    container.exec([ "chmod", "600", "/home/#{username}/.ssh/authorized_keys" ])
   203→  end
   204→
   205→  def create_wetty_container(sandbox:, user:, key_dir:)
   206→    container_name = wetty_container_name(sandbox)
   207→
   208→    # Remove any existing container with this name
   209→    begin
   210→      old = Docker::Container.get(container_name)
   211→      old.stop(t: 3) rescue nil
   212→      old.delete(force: true)
   213→    rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
   214→      # No existing container
   215→    end
   216→
   217→    ssh_command = [
   218→      "ssh", "-p", "22",
   219→      "-o", "StrictHostKeyChecking=no",
   220→      "-o", "UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null",
   221→      "-o", "LogLevel=ERROR",
   222→      "-i", "/etc/wetty/key",
   223→      "#{user.name}@#{sandbox.full_name}",
   224→      "-t", "tmux new-session -A -s main"
   225→    ].join(" ")
   226→
   227→    container = Docker::Container.create(
   228→      "name" => container_name,
   229→      "Image" => WETTY_IMAGE,
   230→      "Env" => [
   231→        "COMMAND=#{ssh_command}",
   232→        "BASE=/terminal/#{sandbox.id}/wetty"
   233→      ],
   234→      "HostConfig" => {
   235→        "NetworkMode" => NETWORK_NAME,
   236→        "RestartPolicy" => { "Name" => "no" },
   237→        "Memory" => 128 * 1024 * 1024, # 128MB
   238→        "NanoCpus" => 500_000_000 # 0.5 CPU
   239→      },
   240→      "Labels" => {
   241→        "sandcastle.sandbox_id" => sandbox.id.to_s,
   242→        "sandcastle.role" => "wetty"
   243→      }
   244→    )
   245→
   246→    container.start
   247→    copy_key_to_container(container, key_dir)
   248→    container
   249→  end
   250→
   251→  # Copy the SSH private key into the WeTTY container via exec + base64.
   252→  # This avoids bind-mount path issues when Rails runs inside a container
   253→  # with a Docker volume for /data (the host path doesn't match).
   254→  def copy_key_to_container(container, key_dir)
   255→    key_content = File.read(File.join(key_dir, "key"))
   256→    encoded = Base64.strict_encode64(key_content)
   257→
   258→    container.exec([ "mkdir", "-p", "/etc/wetty" ])
   259→    container.exec([ "sh", "-c", "echo #{encoded} | base64 -d > /etc/wetty/key" ])
   260→    container.exec([ "chmod", "600", "/etc/wetty/key" ])
   261→  end
   262→
   263→  def write_traefik_config(sandbox)
   264→    FileUtils.mkdir_p(DYNAMIC_DIR)
   265→
   266→    host = ENV.fetch("SANDCASTLE_HOST", "localhost")
   267→    id = sandbox.id
   268→    container_name = wetty_container_name(sandbox)
   269→
   270→    rule = if ENV["SANDCASTLE_TLS_MODE"] == "selfsigned"
   271→      "HostRegexp(`.+`) && PathPrefix(`/terminal/#{id}/wetty`)"
   272→    else
   273→      "Host(`#{host}`) && PathPrefix(`/terminal/#{id}/wetty`)"
   274→    end
   275→
   276→    config = {
   277→      "http" => {
   278→        "routers" => {
   279→          "terminal-#{id}" => {
   280→            "rule" => rule,
   281→            "service" => "terminal-#{id}",
   282→            "entryPoints" => [ "websecure" ],
   283→            "tls" => tls_config,
   284→            "middlewares" => [ "terminal-auth-#{id}" ],
   285→            "priority" => 100
   286→          }
   287→        },
   288→        "middlewares" => {
   289→          "terminal-auth-#{id}" => {
   290→            "forwardAuth" => {
   291→              "address" => "http://sandcastle-web:80/terminal/auth",
   292→              "trustForwardHeader" => true
   293→            }
   294→          }
   295→        },
   296→        "services" => {
   297→          "terminal-#{id}" => {
   298→            "loadBalancer" => {
   299→              "servers" => [ { "url" => "http://#{container_name}:3000" } ]
   300→            }
   301→          }
   302→        }
   303→      }
   304→    }
   305→
   306→    File.write(File.join(DYNAMIC_DIR, "terminal-#{id}.yml"), config.to_yaml)
   307→  end
   308→
   309→  def tls_config
   310→    if ENV["SANDCASTLE_TLS_MODE"] == "selfsigned"
   311→      {}
   312→    else
   313→      { "certResolver" => "letsencrypt" }
   314→    end
   315→  end
   316→
   317→  def delete_traefik_config(sandbox)
   318→    path = File.join(DYNAMIC_DIR, "terminal-#{sandbox.id}.yml")
   319→    File.delete(path) if File.exist?(path)
   320→  end
   321→
   322→  def remove_pubkey(sandbox)
   323→    return unless sandbox.container_id.present?
   324→
   325→    begin
   326→      container = Docker::Container.get(sandbox.container_id)
   327→      username = sandbox.user.name
   328→      # Match end-of-line to avoid substring collisions
   329→      # (e.g. "wetty-user-foo" must not also remove "wetty-user-foobar").
   330→      # Sandbox names are [a-z0-9_-] only, so safe for regex.
   331→      marker = "wetty-#{sandbox.full_name}$"
   332→      container.exec([
   333→        "sh", "-c",
   334→        "grep -v '#{marker}' /home/#{username}/.ssh/authorized_keys > /tmp/ak_clean && " \
   335→        "mv /tmp/ak_clean /home/#{username}/.ssh/authorized_keys || true"
   336→      ])
   337→    rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError, Docker::Error::DockerError
   338→      # Sandbox container gone or exec failed — best-effort
   339→    end
   340→  end
   341→end
   342→
```

```
     4→  <div class="bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-lg p-6">
     5→    <div id="connecting" class="text-center">
     6→      <div class="flex items-center justify-center gap-3 mb-4">
     7→        <svg class="w-5 h-5 text-blue-500 animate-spin" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
     8→          <circle class="opacity-25" cx="12" cy="12" r="10" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="4"></circle>
     9→          <path class="opacity-75" fill="currentColor" d="M4 12a8 8 0 018-8V0C5.373 0 0 5.373 0 12h4z"></path>
    10→        </svg>
    11→        <h2 class="text-lg font-semibold text-gray-900">Connecting to terminal...</h2>
    12→      </div>
    13→      <p class="text-sm text-gray-500">Waiting for the terminal to become ready.</p>
    14→    </div>
    15→
    16→    <div id="timeout-error" class="hidden text-center">
    17→      <div class="mb-4">
    18→        <svg class="w-8 h-8 text-red-500 mx-auto mb-2" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2">
    19→          <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="M12 9v2m0 4h.01M21 12a9 9 0 11-18 0 9 9 0 0118 0z" />
    20→        </svg>
    21→        <h2 class="text-lg font-semibold text-gray-900">Connection timed out</h2>
    22→      </div>
    23→      <p class="text-sm text-gray-500 mb-4">The terminal did not become ready in time. Try closing and reopening it.</p>
    24→      <a href="<%= root_path %>" class="inline-block px-4 py-2 bg-gray-200 text-gray-700 rounded hover:bg-gray-300 text-sm">
    25→        Back to Dashboard
    26→      </a>
    27→    </div>
    28→  </div>
    29→</div>
    30→
    31→<script>
    32→  (function() {
    33→    var statusUrl = "<%= terminal_status_sandbox_path(@sandbox) %>";
    34→    var terminalUrl = "<%= j @terminal_url %>";
    35→    var elapsed = 0;
    36→    var timeout = 30;
    37→
    38→    var poll = setInterval(function() {
    39→      elapsed++;
    40→
    41→      if (elapsed > timeout) {
    42→        clearInterval(poll);
    43→        document.getElementById("connecting").classList.add("hidden");
    44→        document.getElementById("timeout-error").classList.remove("hidden");
    45→        return;
    46→      }
    47→
    48→      fetch(statusUrl, { headers: { "Accept": "application/json" } })
    49→        .then(function(r) { return r.json(); })
    50→        .then(function(data) {
    51→          if (data.status === "ready") {
    52→            clearInterval(poll);
    53→            window.location.href = terminalUrl;
    54→          }
    55→        })
    56→        .catch(function() {});
    57→    }, 1000);
    58→  })();
    59→</script>
    60→
```

     1→# VNC Debug Session - 2026-02-15
     2→
     3→## Issues Found & Fixed
     4→
     5→### 1. Port Mismatch in VncManager ✅ FIXED
     6→
     7→**Problem:** VncManager was trying to connect to noVNC container on port 8080, but noVNC actually listens on port 6080.
     8→
     9→**Fix:** Updated `app/services/vnc_manager.rb:223`
    10→
    11→```ruby
    12→# Before:
    13→"servers" => [ { "url" => "http://#{container_name}:8080" } ]
    14→
    15→# After:
    16→"servers" => [ { "url" => "http://#{container_name}:6080" } ]
    17→```
    18→
    19→**Also updated:** Existing Traefik config `/data/traefik/dynamic/vnc-38.yml` to use port 6080.
    20→
    21→### 2. Missing VNC Server in Sandbox Container ⏳ PENDING
    22→
    23→**Problem:** Running sandbox containers use old image (`ghcr.io/thieso2/sandcastle-sandbox:latest`) that doesn't have VNC tools (Xvfb, x11vnc) installed.
    24→
    25→**Root Cause:** The sandbox Dockerfile includes VNC installation, but deployed containers are using an older image version built before VNC support was added.
    26→
    27→**Fix Applied:** Updated `images/sandbox/Dockerfile` to install VNC tools:
    28→
    29→```dockerfile
    30→# GUI tools: Xvfb (virtual X server) and x11vnc for browser access
    31→RUN apt-get update && apt-get install -y \
    32→    xvfb x11vnc xfonts-base xfonts-100dpi xfonts-75dpi \
    33→    xterm fluxbox \
    34→    && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*
    35→```
    36→
    37→**Note:** Chrome installation commented out due to dependency conflicts with Ubuntu 25.10.
    38→
    39→### 3. Traefik Configuration ✅ WORKING
    40→
    41→**Status:** Traefik dynamic config exists and is properly configured. The VNC routing works correctly after the port fix.
    42→
    43→## What Works
    44→
    45→- ✅ noVNC container runs and listens on port 6080
    46→- ✅ Traefik routing configured with forwardAuth middleware
    47→- ✅ Authentication flow (`VncController#auth`) works correctly
    48→- ✅ VncManager service logic is sound (after port fix)
    49→- ✅ Web container restarted to apply code changes
    50→
    51→## What's Missing
    52→
    53→- ⏳ VNC server (Xvfb + x11vnc) running in sandbox containers
    54→- ⏳ Rebuild sandbox image with VNC tools
    55→- ⏳ Recreate sandbox with new image
    56→
    57→## Next Steps to Enable VNC
    58→
    59→### 1. Rebuild Sandbox Image
    60→
    61→```bash
    62→cd images/sandbox
    63→docker build -t sandcastle-sandbox:latest .
    64→```
    65→
    66→**Note:** This may take 10-15 minutes due to Docker installation step. Network speed affects build time.
    67→
    68→### 2. Verify VNC Tools in Image
    69→
    70→```bash
    71→docker run --rm sandcastle-sandbox:latest bash -c \
    72→  'command -v Xvfb && command -v x11vnc && echo "✓ VNC tools installed"'
    73→```
    74→
    75→Should output:
    76→```
    77→/usr/bin/Xvfb
    78→/usr/bin/x11vnc
    79→✓ VNC tools installed
    80→```
    81→
    82→### 3. Recreate Sandbox with New Image
    83→
    84→Via Rails console:
    85→
    86→```bash
    87→docker exec -it sandcastle-web ./bin/rails runner "
    88→  sandbox = Sandbox.find_by(name: 'quantum-viper')
    89→  if sandbox
    90→    user = sandbox.user
    91→    persistent = sandbox.persistent_volume
    92→
    93→    # Destroy old sandbox
    94→    SandboxManager.new.destroy(sandbox: sandbox)
    95→
    96→    # Create new sandbox with updated image
    97→    SandboxManager.new.create(
    98→      user: user,
    99→      name: 'quantum-viper',
   100→      image: 'sandcastle-sandbox:latest',
   101→      persistent: persistent
   102→    )
   103→  end
   104→"
   105→```
   106→
   107→Or via web UI:
   108→1. Delete the sandbox
   109→2. Create a new one with the same name
   110→
   111→### 4. Test VNC Connection
   112→
   113→1. Click the browser icon for the sandbox in the web UI
   114→2. Wait for the "Connecting to browser..." page
   115→3. Should redirect to noVNC interface showing the desktop
   116→
   117→## Technical Details
   118→
   119→### VNC Architecture
   120→
   121→```
   122→Browser
   123→  ↓ HTTPS
   124→Traefik (:8443)
   125→  ↓ forwardAuth → VncController#auth (validates session)
   126→  ↓ stripPrefix (/vnc/{id}/novnc)
   127→noVNC Container (port 6080)
   128→  ↓ VNC protocol (port 5900)
   129→Sandbox Container
   130→  ├─ Xvfb :99 (virtual X display)
   131→  └─ x11vnc -display :99 -rfbport 5900
   132→```
   133→
   134→### Container Names
   135→
   136→- noVNC: `sc-vnc-{user}-{sandbox}` (e.g., `sc-vnc-thies-quantum-viper`)
   137→- Sandbox: `{user}-{sandbox}` (e.g., `thies-quantum-viper`)
   138→- Network: `sandcastle-web` (bridge)
   139→
   140→### VNC Server Startup
   141→
   142→The sandbox entrypoint (`images/sandbox/entrypoint.sh:71-80`) automatically starts:
   143→
   144→1. **Xvfb** on display :99 with 1920x1080 resolution
   145→2. **x11vnc** listening on port 5900, connected to display :99
   146→
   147→```bash
   148→Xvfb :99 -screen 0 1920x1080x24 &>/var/log/xvfb.log &
   149→sleep 1
   150→DISPLAY=:99 x11vnc -shared -forever -nopw -rfbport 5900 &>/var/log/x11vnc.log &
   151→```
   152→
   153→### Debugging Commands
   154→
   155→```bash
   156→# Check if noVNC container is running
   157→docker ps | grep sc-vnc
   158→
   159→# Check noVNC logs
   160→docker logs sc-vnc-thies-quantum-viper
   161→
   162→# Check if VNC server is running in sandbox
   163→docker exec thies-quantum-viper ps aux | grep -E "Xvfb|x11vnc"
   164→
   165→# Check VNC server logs
   166→docker exec thies-quantum-viper cat /var/log/xvfb.log
   167→docker exec thies-quantum-viper cat /var/log/x11vnc.log
   168→
   169→# Test VNC connection from sandbox to itself
   170→docker exec thies-quantum-viper nc -zv localhost 5900
   171→
   172→# Check Traefik config
   173→docker exec sandcastle-web cat /data/traefik/dynamic/vnc-38.yml
   174→
   175→# Check noVNC is listening on port 6080
   176→docker exec sc-vnc-thies-quantum-viper netstat -tlnp | grep 6080
   177→```
   178→
   179→## Files Modified
   180→
   181→1. `app/services/vnc_manager.rb` - Port fix (8080 → 6080)
   182→2. `images/sandbox/Dockerfile` - Added VNC tools installation
   183→3. `/data/traefik/dynamic/vnc-38.yml` - Runtime config update (port fix)
   184→
   185→## Known Issues
   186→
   187→### Ubuntu 25.10 + Chrome Compatibility
   188→
   189→Chrome installation fails on Ubuntu 25.10 due to missing dependencies:
   190→- `libatspi2.0-0:amd64`
   191→- `libxdamage1:amd64`
   192→- `libxext6:amd64`
   193→- etc.
   194→
   195→**Workaround:** Chrome installation commented out in Dockerfile. Users can install it manually if needed, or we can switch to Ubuntu 24.04 LTS for better package compatibility.
   196→
   197→### Slow Package Downloads
   198→
   199→During testing, apt-get package downloads were extremely slow (20+ minutes for a few packages). This affected both image builds and in-container installations.
   200→
   201→**Recommendation:** Run builds during better network conditions or consider using Ubuntu package mirrors closer to your location.
   202→
   203→## Testing Checklist
   204→
   205→Once sandbox is recreated with new image:
   206→
   207→- [ ] VNC button in web UI triggers `/sandboxes/{id}/vnc` POST
   208→- [ ] Redirects to `/sandboxes/{id}/vnc/wait` waiting page
   209→- [ ] Status polling (`/sandboxes/{id}/vnc/status`) returns "ready"
   210→- [ ] Redirects to `/vnc/{id}/novnc` URL
   211→- [ ] Traefik forwardAuth validates session
   212→- [ ] noVNC interface loads in browser
   213→- [ ] Can see Xvfb desktop (black screen is normal - no apps running)
   214→- [ ] Can interact with desktop (right-click, open xterm, etc.)
   215→
   216→## Additional Notes
   217→
   218→- Web container was restarted to pick up VncManager code changes
   219→- Existing VNC session for sandbox #38 should be closed and reopened after sandbox recreation
   220→- The web container restart means the code fix is already live in the local environment
   221→

```
     1→class TerminalOpenJob < ApplicationJob
     2→  queue_as :default
     3→
     4→  def perform(sandbox_id:)
     5→    sandbox = Sandbox.find(sandbox_id)
     6→    return unless sandbox.status == "running"
     7→
     8→    sandbox.start_job("opening_terminal")
     9→
    10→    begin
    11→      TerminalManager.new.open(sandbox: sandbox)
    12→      sandbox.finish_job
    13→    rescue => e
    14→      Rails.logger.error("TerminalOpenJob failed: #{e.message}\n#{e.backtrace.join("\n")}")
    15→      sandbox.fail_job("Failed to open terminal: #{e.message}")
    16→      # Don't re-raise - terminal failures shouldn't retry
    17→    end
    18→  end
    19→end
    20→
```

```
     1→class Sandbox < ApplicationRecord
     2→  include ActionView::RecordIdentifier  # For dom_id in Turbo broadcasts
     3→
     4→  belongs_to :user
     5→  has_many :routes, dependent: :destroy
     6→
     7→  SSH_PORT_RANGE = (2201..2299)
     8→  VNC_GEOMETRIES = %w[1280x900 1366x768 1440x900 1600x900 1920x1080 2560x1440].freeze
     9→  VNC_DEPTHS = [ 8, 16, 24, 32 ].freeze
    10→
    11→  validates :name, presence: true,
    12→    uniqueness: { scope: :user_id, conditions: -> { where.not(status: "destroyed") } },
    13→    format: { with: /\A[a-z][a-z0-9_-]{0,62}\z/, message: "must be lowercase alphanumeric" }
    14→  validates :ssh_port, presence: true,
    15→    uniqueness: { conditions: -> { where.not(status: "destroyed") } },
    16→    inclusion: { in: SSH_PORT_RANGE }
    17→  validates :status, inclusion: { in: %w[pending running stopped destroyed] }
    18→  validates :image, presence: true
    19→  validates :vnc_geometry, inclusion: { in: VNC_GEOMETRIES }
    20→  validates :vnc_depth, inclusion: { in: VNC_DEPTHS }
    21→
    22→  scope :active, -> { where.not(status: "destroyed") }
    23→  scope :running, -> { where(status: "running") }
    24→
    25→  before_validation :assign_ssh_port, on: :create
    26→
    27→  # Turbo Streams for real-time UI updates
    28→  after_create_commit :broadcast_prepend_to_dashboard
    29→  after_update_commit :broadcast_replace_to_dashboard
    30→  after_destroy_commit :broadcast_remove_from_dashboard
    31→
    32→  def full_name
    33→    "#{user.name}-#{name}"
    34→  end
    35→
    36→  def connect_command(host: ENV.fetch("SANDCASTLE_HOST", "localhost"))
    37→    "ssh -p #{ssh_port} #{user.name}@#{host}"
    38→  end
    39→
    40→  def routed?
    41→    routes.loaded? ? routes.any? : routes.exists?
    42→  end
    43→
    44→  def temp?
    45→    temporary?
    46→  end
    47→
    48→  # Job lifecycle management
    49→  def job_in_progress?
    50→    job_status.present?
    51→  end
    52→
    53→  def job_failed?
    54→    job_error.present?
    55→  end
    56→
    57→  def start_job(status)
    58→    update!(job_status: status, job_started_at: Time.current, job_error: nil)
    59→  end
    60→
    61→  def finish_job
    62→    update!(job_status: nil, job_started_at: nil)
    63→  end
    64→
    65→  def fail_job(error_message)
    66→    update!(job_status: nil, job_error: error_message, job_started_at: nil)
    67→  end
    68→
    69→  private
    70→
    71→  def broadcast_prepend_to_dashboard
    72→    broadcast_prepend_to(
    73→      [user, "dashboard"],
    74→      partial: "dashboard/sandbox",
    75→      locals: { sandbox: self },
    76→      target: "sandboxes"
    77→    )
    78→  end
    79→
    80→  def broadcast_replace_to_dashboard
    81→    # If sandbox is destroyed, remove it from the dashboard instead of replacing
    82→    if status == "destroyed"
    83→      broadcast_remove_to([user, "dashboard"], target: dom_id(self))
    84→    else
    85→      broadcast_replace_to(
    86→        [user, "dashboard"],
    87→        partial: "dashboard/sandbox",
    88→        locals: { sandbox: self },
    89→        target: dom_id(self)
    90→      )
    91→    end
    92→  end
    93→
    94→  def broadcast_remove_from_dashboard
    95→    broadcast_remove_to([user, "dashboard"], target: dom_id(self))
    96→  end
    97→
    98→  def assign_ssh_port
    99→    return if ssh_port.present?
   100→
   101→    used_ports = Sandbox.where.not(status: "destroyed").pluck(:ssh_port)
   102→    available = SSH_PORT_RANGE.to_a - used_ports
   103→    self.ssh_port = available.first || raise("No SSH ports available")
   104→  end
   105→end
   106→
```

```
     1→class SandboxesController < ApplicationController
     2→  before_action :set_sandbox, only: [ :destroy, :start, :stop, :retry ]
     3→
     4→  def new
     5→    authorize Sandbox
     6→    @snapshots = SandboxManager.new.list_snapshots(user: Current.user)
     7→  end
     8→
     9→  def create
    10→    authorize Sandbox
    11→
    12→    image = if params[:snapshot].present?
    13→      "sc-snap-#{Current.user.name}:#{params[:snapshot]}"
    14→    else
    15→      params[:image].presence || SandboxManager::DEFAULT_IMAGE
    16→    end
    17→
    18→    # Build sandbox record
    19→    # Note: temporary sandboxes can only be created via CLI
    20→    sandbox = Current.user.sandboxes.build(
    21→      name: params.require(:name),
    22→      status: "pending",
    23→      image: image,
    24→      persistent_volume: params[:persistent] == "1",
    25→      mount_home: params[:mount_home] == "1",
    26→      data_path: params[:data_path].presence,
    27→      tailscale: params[:tailscale] == "1",
    28→      vnc_enabled: params[:vnc_enabled] != "0",
    29→      vnc_geometry: Sandbox::VNC_GEOMETRIES.include?(params[:vnc_geometry]) ? params[:vnc_geometry] : "1280x900",
    30→      vnc_depth: Sandbox::VNC_DEPTHS.include?(params[:vnc_depth].to_i) ? params[:vnc_depth].to_i : 24,
    31→      temporary: false
    32→    )
    33→
    34→    if sandbox.persistent_volume
    35→      sandbox.volume_path = "#{SandboxManager::DATA_DIR}/sandboxes/#{sandbox.full_name}/vol"
    36→    end
    37→
    38→    if sandbox.save
    39→      # Enqueue async job
    40→      SandboxProvisionJob.perform_later(sandbox_id: sandbox.id)
    41→
    42→      # Optimistic redirect - dashboard will update via Turbo
    43→      redirect_to root_path, notice: "Creating sandcastle #{sandbox.name}..."
    44→    else
    45→      @snapshots = SandboxManager.new.list_snapshots(user: Current.user)
    46→      flash.now[:alert] = "Failed to create sandbox: #{sandbox.errors.full_messages.join(', ')}"
    47→      render :new, status: :unprocessable_entity
    48→    end
    49→  rescue => e
    50→    @snapshots = SandboxManager.new.list_snapshots(user: Current.user)
    51→    flash.now[:alert] = "Failed to create sandbox: #{e.message}"
    52→    render :new, status: :unprocessable_entity
    53→  end
    54→
    55→  def destroy
    56→    if @sandbox.job_in_progress?
    57→      redirect_to root_path, alert: "Operation already in progress"
    58→      return
    59→    end
    60→
    61→    @sandbox.start_job("destroying")
    62→    SandboxDestroyJob.perform_later(sandbox_id: @sandbox.id)
    63→
    64→    respond_to do |format|
    65→      format.html { redirect_to root_path, notice: "Destroying sandcastle #{@sandbox.name}..." }
    66→      format.turbo_stream { render turbo_stream: turbo_stream.replace(@sandbox, partial: "dashboard/sandbox", locals: { sandbox: @sandbox }) }
    67→    end
    68→  end
    69→
    70→  def start
    71→    if @sandbox.job_in_progress?
    72→      redirect_to root_path, alert: "Operation already in progress"
    73→      return
    74→    end
    75→
    76→    @sandbox.start_job("starting")
    77→    SandboxStartJob.perform_later(sandbox_id: @sandbox.id)
    78→
    79→    respond_to do |format|
    80→      format.html { redirect_to root_path, notice: "Starting sandcastle #{@sandbox.name}..." }
    81→      format.turbo_stream { render turbo_stream: turbo_stream.replace(@sandbox, partial: "dashboard/sandbox", locals: { sandbox: @sandbox }) }
    82→    end
    83→  end
    84→
    85→  def stop
    86→    if @sandbox.job_in_progress?
    87→      redirect_to root_path, alert: "Operation already in progress"
    88→      return
    89→    end
    90→
    91→    @sandbox.start_job("stopping")
    92→    SandboxStopJob.perform_later(sandbox_id: @sandbox.id)
    93→
    94→    respond_to do |format|
    95→      format.html { redirect_to root_path, notice: "Stopping sandcastle #{@sandbox.name}..." }
    96→      format.turbo_stream { render turbo_stream: turbo_stream.replace(@sandbox, partial: "dashboard/sandbox", locals: { sandbox: @sandbox }) }
    97→    end
    98→  end
    99→
   100→  def retry
   101→    return unless @sandbox.job_failed?
   102→
   103→    @sandbox.update!(job_error: nil)
   104→
   105→    case @sandbox.status
   106→    when "destroyed", "pending"
   107→      redirect_to root_path, alert: "Cannot retry creation. Please create a new sandbox."
   108→    when "stopped"
   109→      SandboxStartJob.perform_later(sandbox_id: @sandbox.id)
   110→      redirect_to root_path, notice: "Retrying start..."
   111→    when "running"
   112→      redirect_to root_path, alert: "Sandbox is already running"
   113→    end
   114→  end
   115→
   116→  private
   117→
   118→  def set_sandbox
   119→    @sandbox = policy_scope(Sandbox).find(params[:id])
   120→    authorize @sandbox
   121→  end
   122→end
```

```
     4→  def perform(sandbox_id:)
```

> AGENT

Now I have the full picture. There are two issues:

1. **The `TerminalController#open` calls `TerminalManager.new.open` directly (synchronous)** while every other operation (start/stop/destroy/create) uses async jobs. The `TerminalOpenJob` exists but is never called from the controller.

2. **Idempotent Traefik config bug**: When the WeTTY container is already running, `open` returns early and skips `write_traefik_config`. If the config was deleted by a previous `close`, Traefik has no route for `/terminal/40/wetty` → it falls through to the Rails catch-all → 404.

The 21ms completion = idempotent early return (WeTTY container already running from a previous session). The Traefik config is gone. Browser gets a 404.

```
    56→  # Build the full terminal URL. In production, Traefik is the entry point
    57→  # so a relative path works. In local dev (selfsigned TLS), Rails may be
    58→  # accessed directly on a different port, so we need an absolute URL.
    59→  def terminal_redirect_url(path)
    60→    base = ENV["SANDCASTLE_TERMINAL_URL"]
    61→    base ? "#{base}#{path}" : path
    62→  end
    63→
    64→  public
    65→
    66→  # Called by Traefik forwardAuth. Returns 200 to allow, or redirects to
    67→  # login (which Traefik passes through to the browser).
    68→  def auth
    69→    forwarded_uri = request.headers["X-Forwarded-Uri"] || ""
    70→    match = forwarded_uri.match(%r{/terminal/(\d+)/wetty})
    71→    head(:unauthorized) and return unless match
    72→
    73→    session_record = find_session_by_cookie
    74→    unless session_record
    75→      # Build the original terminal URL from Traefik's forwarded headers
    76→      # so the user returns here after logging in.
    77→      proto = request.headers["X-Forwarded-Proto"] || "https"
    78→      host  = request.headers["X-Forwarded-Host"] || request.host_with_port
    79→      original_url = "#{proto}://#{host}#{forwarded_uri}"
    80→      session[:return_to_after_authenticating] = original_url
    81→      redirect_to new_session_url, allow_other_host: true
    82→      return
    83→    end
    84→
    85→    user = session_record.user
    86→    sandbox = Sandbox.active.find_by(id: match[1].to_i)
    87→    head(:unauthorized) and return unless sandbox && (sandbox.user_id == user.id || user.admin?)
    88→
    89→    head :ok
    90→  end
    91→end
```

> AGENT

The web container stopped gracefully — the user must have brought the stack down after testing. The status polled once, returned 200.
```
[tasks.dev]
description = "Create/migrate DB if needed, then start the dev server"
run = """
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail
bin/rails db:prepare
bin/dev
"""

[tasks."deploy:dev"]
description = "Run local development with live source mounting"
run = """
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

echo "Starting development environment with live reload..."
docker compose -f docker-compose.dev.yml up --build

echo ""
echo "Development server running at:"
echo "  - https://localhost:8443 (via Traefik)"
echo "  - http://localhost:8080 (via Traefik)"
echo ""
echo "Changes to Ruby/ERB files will reload automatically"
echo "Tailwind CSS compiles on file changes"
echo ""
echo "Logs: docker compose -f docker-compose.dev.yml logs -f"
"""

[tasks."deploy:dev:down"]
description = "Stop development docker-compose deployment"
run = "docker compose -f docker-compose.dev.yml down"

[tasks."deploy:dev:reset"]
description = "Reset development docker-compose environment"
run = """
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

echo "Removing WeTTY containers..."
docker ps -a --filter "name=sc-wetty-" -q | xargs -r docker rm -f || true

echo "Removing Tailscale containers..."
docker ps -a --filter "name=sc-ts-" -q | xargs -r docker rm -f || true

echo "Stopping and removing containers, volumes, and networks..."
docker compose -f docker-compose.dev.yml down --volumes --remove-orphans

echo ""
echo "Development environment reset complete."
echo "Run 'mise run deploy:dev' to rebuild and start fresh."
"""

[tasks."deploy:local"]
description = "Build image and deploy locally via docker-compose.local.yml"
run = """
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

export BUILD_VERSION=$(git describe --tags --abbrev=0 2>/dev/null || echo "dev")
export BUILD_GIT_SHA=$(git rev-parse --short HEAD 2>/dev/null || echo "unknown")
export BUILD_GIT_DIRTY=$([ -z "$(git status --porcelain 2>/dev/null)" ] && echo "" || echo "-dirty")
export BUILD_DATE=$(date -u +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ)

echo "Building sandcastle:local ${BUILD_VERSION} (${BUILD_GIT_SHA}${BUILD_GIT_DIRTY})..."
docker compose -f docker-compose.local.yml build

echo "Starting services..."
docker compose -f docker-compose.local.yml up

echo ""
echo "Sandcastle running at:"
echo "  - https://localhost:8443 (via Traefik, with terminal support)"
echo "  - http://localhost:8080 (via Traefik, HTTP)"
echo ""
echo "Logs: docker compose -f docker-compose.local.yml logs -f"
"""

[tasks."deploy:local:down"]
description = "Stop local docker-compose deployment"
run = "docker compose -f docker-compose.local.yml down"

[tasks."deploy:local:reset"]
description = "Reset local docker-compose environment (stop, remove volumes, rebuild)"
run = """
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

echo "Removing WeTTY containers..."
docker ps -a --filter "name=sc-wetty-" -q | xargs -r docker rm -f || true

echo "Removing Tailscale containers..."
docker ps -a --filter "name=sc-ts-" -q | xargs -r docker rm -f || true

echo "Stopping and removing containers, volumes, and networks..."
docker compose -f docker-compose.local.yml down --volumes --remove-orphans

echo ""
echo "Local environment reset complete."
echo "Run 'mise run deploy:local' to rebuild and start fresh."
"""

[tasks."deploy:local:logs"]
description = "Tail logs from local docker-compose deployment"
run = "docker compose -f docker-compose.local.yml logs -f"

[tasks."release"]
alias = "release:patch"
description = "Bump patch version, tag, push, and wait for CI to build the release"
run = """
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

# Check if HEAD already has a tag
if git describe --exact-match --tags HEAD 2>/dev/null; then
  echo "ERROR: Current commit already has a tag. Make new commits before releasing."
  exit 1
fi

# Get the latest tag
latest=$(git describe --tags --abbrev=0 2>/dev/null || echo "v0.0.0")
echo "Current version: $latest"

# Parse and bump patch
IFS='.' read -r major minor patch <<< "${latest#v}"
new_version="v${major}.${minor}.$((patch + 1))"
echo "New version:     $new_version"

# Confirm
read -rp "Release $new_version? [y/N] " confirm
[[ "$confirm" =~ ^[Yy]$ ]] || { echo "Aborted."; exit 1; }

# Tag and push
git tag "$new_version"
git push origin "$new_version"
echo ""
echo "Tag $new_version pushed. GitHub Actions will build the release."
echo "https://github.com/thieso2/Sandcastle/actions"
"""

[tasks."release:minor"]
description = "Bump minor version, tag, push, and wait for CI to build the release"
run = """
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

# Check if HEAD already has a tag
if git describe --exact-match --tags HEAD 2>/dev/null; then
  echo "ERROR: Current commit already has a tag. Make new commits before releasing."
  exit 1
fi

latest=$(git describe --tags --abbrev=0 2>/dev/null || echo "v0.0.0")
echo "Current version: $latest"

IFS='.' read -r major minor patch <<< "${latest#v}"
new_version="v${major}.$((minor + 1)).0"
echo "New version:     $new_version"

read -rp "Release $new_version? [y/N] " confirm
[[ "$confirm" =~ ^[Yy]$ ]] || { echo "Aborted."; exit 1; }

git tag "$new_version"
git push origin "$new_version"
echo ""
echo "Tag $new_version pushed. GitHub Actions will build the release."
echo "https://github.com/thieso2/Sandcastle/actions"
"""

[tasks."release:major"]
description = "Bump major version, tag, push, and wait for CI to build the release"
run = """
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

# Check if HEAD already has a tag
if git describe --exact-match --tags HEAD 2>/dev/null; then
  echo "ERROR: Current commit already has a tag. Make new commits before releasing."
  exit 1
fi

latest=$(git describe --tags --abbrev=0 2>/dev/null || echo "v0.0.0")
echo "Current version: $latest"

IFS='.' read -r major minor patch <<< "${latest#v}"
new_version="v$((major + 1)).0.0"
echo "New version:     $new_version"

read -rp "Release $new_version? [y/N] " confirm
[[ "$confirm" =~ ^[Yy]$ ]] || { echo "Aborted."; exit 1; }

git tag "$new_version"
git push origin "$new_version"
echo ""
echo "Tag $new_version pushed. GitHub Actions will build the release."
echo "https://github.com/thieso2/Sandcastle/actions"
"""

[tasks."installer:build"]
description = "Build installer.sh from installer.sh.in template and source files"
run = "installer/build.sh"

[tasks."installer:validate"]
description = "Validate that installer.sh matches the built version"
run = """
#!/bin/bash
set -euo pipefail
TEMP=$(mktemp)
installer/build.sh > /dev/null 2>&1
cp installer.sh "$TEMP"
if diff -q installer.sh "$TEMP" >/dev/null; then
  echo "✓ installer.sh is up to date"
  rm "$TEMP"
  exit 0
else
  echo "✗ installer.sh is out of sync! Run: mise run installer:build"
  rm "$TEMP"
  exit 1
fi
"""
```

> AGENT

Found the root cause. `SANDCASTLE_TERMINAL_URL: https://sandcastle.local:8443` — but the user is accessing via `localhost:8080`. When status returns "ready", the JS redirects to `https://sandcastle.local:8443/terminal/40/wetty`. If `sandcastle.local` doesn't resolve on your Mac, the browser hits "name not resolved" — that's why WeTTY shows zero connections.

The fix: change the terminal URL to `localhost` in `docker-compose.local.yml`:

> TOOL

tool_use Read
id: toolu_017Fz15QpCAmwQmheXVKkJ1P
```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/docker-compose.local.yml"
}
```

> AGENT

The volume has `HostRegexp(`.+`)` from a previous `deploy:dev` run — Traefik routes any host to Rails. But `SANDCASTLE_TERMINAL_URL` sends the browser to `https://sandcastle.local:8443` which won't resolve when accessing via `localhost`. That's why WeTTY gets zero connections.

Fix: change `SANDCASTLE_TERMINAL_URL` to `localhost` in `docker-compose.local.yml`:

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_01GNzYMEaFxAVd31SgG1mkXs
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/docker-compose.local.yml",
  "old_string": "      SANDCASTLE_TERMINAL_URL: https://sandcastle.local:8443",
  "new_string": "      SANDCASTLE_TERMINAL_URL: https://localhost:8443"
}
```

Now let's also revert my earlier terminal controller changes — they're unnecessary overhead and would break the terminal if the worker happens to be down. The terminal should stay **synchronous** (it was working fine before, and the real bug was just the URL mismatch):

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_01A9LoP3FKH6f4z5XPwsaZtq
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/app/controllers/terminal_controller.rb",
  "old_string": "  def open\n    sandbox = find_sandbox\n\n    if sandbox.job_in_progress?\n      redirect_to terminal_wait_sandbox_path(sandbox), status: :see_other\n      return\n    end\n\n    sandbox.start_job(\"opening_terminal\")\n    TerminalOpenJob.perform_later(sandbox_id: sandbox.id)\n    redirect_to terminal_wait_sandbox_path(sandbox), status: :see_other\n  rescue TerminalManager::Error => e\n    redirect_to root_path, alert: e.message\n  end",
  "new_string": "  def open\n    sandbox = find_sandbox\n\n    TerminalManager.new.open(sandbox: sandbox)\n    redirect_to terminal_wait_sandbox_path(sandbox), status: :see_other\n  rescue TerminalManager::Error => e\n    redirect_to root_path, alert: e.message\n  end"
}
```

> AGENT

Stack is up. Now try opening the terminal from the browser at `https://localhost:8443` (or `http://localhost:8080`). To summarize what was found and fixed:

**Root cause:** `SANDCASTLE_TERMINAL_URL` in `docker-compose.local.yml` was `https://sandcastle.local:8443`. When the wait page JS got `"ready"`, it redirected the browser to `https://sandcastle.local:8443/terminal/40/wetty`. That hostname doesn't resolve on your Mac (you're accessing via `localhost`), so the browser failed silently — WeTTY received zero connections.

**Fixes applied:**
1. **`docker-compose.local.yml`**: Changed `SANDCASTLE_TERMINAL_URL` from `https://sandcastle.local:8443` → `https://localhost:8443`
2. **`app/services/terminal_manager.rb`**: In the idempotent path (WeTTY container already running), now always rewrites the Traefik config — prevents a stale/deleted config from breaking repeat opens

```
    79→  def write_rails_config(host:)
    80→    FileUtils.mkdir_p(DYNAMIC_DIR)
    81→
    82→    # Build host list: main host + optional alternative hostnames
    83→    hosts = [ host ]
    84→    alt_hostnames = ENV["SANDCASTLE_ALT_HOSTNAMES"].to_s.split(",").map(&:strip).reject(&:empty?)
    85→    hosts += alt_hostnames
    86→
    87→    rule = if ENV["SANDCASTLE_TLS_MODE"] == "selfsigned"
    88→      "HostRegexp(`.+`)"
    89→    else
    90→      host_rules = hosts.map { |h| "`#{h}`" }.join(", ")
    91→      "Host(#{host_rules})"
    92→    end
    93→
    94→    config = {
    95→      "http" => {
    96→        "routers" => {
    97→          "rails-http" => {
    98→            "rule" => rule,
    99→            "service" => "rails",
   100→            "entryPoints" => [ "web" ]
   101→          },
   102→          "rails-https" => {
   103→            "rule" => rule,
   104→            "service" => "rails",
   105→            "entryPoints" => [ "websecure" ],
   106→            "tls" => tls_config
   107→          }
   108→        },
   109→        "services" => {
   110→          "rails" => {
   111→            "loadBalancer" => {
   112→              "servers" => [ { "url" => "http://sandcastle-web:80" } ]
   113→            }
   114→          }
   115→        }
   116→      }
   117→    }
   118→
   119→    # Add TLS certificates section for self-signed mode
   120→    if ENV["SANDCASTLE_TLS_MODE"] == "selfsigned"
   121→      certs = [
   122→        {
   123→          "certFile" => "/data/certs/cert.pem",
   124→          "keyFile" => "/data/certs/key.pem"
   125→        }
   126→      ]
   127→
   128→      # Add custom certificates if configured
   129→      if custom_cert_configured?
   130→        certs << {
   131→          "certFile" => "/data/certs/custom-cert.pem",
   132→          "keyFile" => "/data/certs/custom-key.pem"
   133→        }
   134→      end
   135→
   136→      config["tls"] = { "certificates" => certs }
   137→    end
   138→
   139→    File.write(File.join(DYNAMIC_DIR, "rails.yml"), config.to_yaml)
   140→  end
   141→
   142→  private
   143→
   144→  def custom_cert_configured?
   145→    cert_path = File.join(DATA_DIR, "traefik", "certs", "custom-cert.pem")
   146→    key_path = File.join(DATA_DIR, "traefik", "certs", "custom-key.pem")
   147→    File.exist?(cert_path) && File.exist?(key_path)
   148→  end
   149→
   150→  def config_path(sandbox)
   151→    File.join(DYNAMIC_DIR, "sandbox-#{sandbox.id}.yml")
   152→  end
   153→
   154→  def write_config(sandbox)
   155→    FileUtils.mkdir_p(DYNAMIC_DIR)
   156→
   157→    routes = sandbox.routes.reload
   158→    routers = {}
   159→    services = {}
   160→
   161→    routes.each do |route|
   162→      key = "sandbox-#{sandbox.id}-r#{route.id}"
   163→      routers[key] = {
   164→        "rule" => "Host(`#{route.domain}`)",
   165→        "service" => key,
   166→        "entryPoints" => [ "websecure" ],
   167→        "tls" => tls_config
   168→      }
   169→      services[key] = {
   170→        "loadBalancer" => {
   171→          "servers" => [ { "url" => "http://#{sandbox.full_name}:#{route.port}" } ]
   172→        }
   173→      }
   174→    end
   175→
   176→    config = { "http" => { "routers" => routers, "services" => services } }
   177→    File.write(config_path(sandbox), config.to_yaml)
   178→  end
   179→
   180→  def tls_config
   181→    if ENV["SANDCASTLE_TLS_MODE"] == "selfsigned"
   182→      {}
   183→    else
   184→      { "certResolver" => "letsencrypt" }
   185→    end
   186→  end
   187→
   188→  def delete_config(sandbox)
   189→    path = config_path(sandbox)
   190→    File.delete(path) if File.exist?(path)
   191→  end
   192→
   193→  def ensure_network
   194→    Docker::Network.get(NETWORK_NAME)
   195→  rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
   196→    Docker::Network.create(NETWORK_NAME, "Driver" => "bridge")
   197→  end
   198→
   199→  def connect_to_network(sandbox)
   200→    return unless sandbox.container_id.present?
   201→
   202→    network = Docker::Network.get(NETWORK_NAME)
   203→    container = Docker::Container.get(sandbox.container_id)
   204→
   205→    # Check if already connected
   206→    networks = container.json.dig("NetworkSettings", "Networks") || {}
   207→    return if networks.key?(NETWORK_NAME)
   208→
   209→    network.connect(sandbox.container_id)
   210→  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError => e
   211→    raise Error, "Failed to connect container to network: #{e.message}"
   212→  end
   213→
   214→  def disconnect_from_network(sandbox)
   215→    return unless sandbox.container_id.present?
   216→
   217→    network = Docker::Network.get(NETWORK_NAME)
   218→    network.disconnect(sandbox.container_id)
   219→  rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
   220→    # Network or container already gone
   221→  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError
   222→    # Container may not be connected
   223→  end
   224→end
   225→
```

> AGENT

Everything is correct:
- Certs exist ✓
- `rails.yml` uses `HostRegexp(`.+`)` → matches `localhost` ✓
- Stack is fully up ✓

The terminal should now work. Try opening it from `http://localhost:8080`. When it redirects to `https://localhost:8443/terminal/40/wetty`, you may get a browser cert warning (self-signed cert was generated for `sandcastle.local`, not `localhost`) — just accept/bypass it.

**Summary of what was wrong and what was fixed:**

| Issue | Fix |
|---|---|
| `SANDCASTLE_TERMINAL_URL: https://sandcastle.local:8443` — browser can't resolve `sandcastle.local` so it never reaches Traefik/WeTTY | Changed to `https://localhost:8443` in `docker-compose.local.yml` |
| Idempotent path in `TerminalManager#open` skipped writing Traefik config when WeTTY container already existed | Added `write_traefik_config(sandbox)` to the idempotent early-return path |

> AGENT

The `migrate` container doesn't mount the Docker socket, but `container_sync.rb` runs `ContainerSyncJob.perform_now` on startup in all environments. It's rescued so migration still works, but it's noisy. The fix: check Docker socket availability first.

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_01Vc4Rvh159wKi8MwSXv1z3M
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/config/initializers/container_sync.rb",
  "old_string": "Rails.application.config.after_initialize do\n  # Only run in production/development, skip in test environment\n  unless Rails.env.test?\n    begin\n      # Run container sync to verify all sandboxes\n      # This will mark any missing containers as destroyed\n      ContainerSyncJob.perform_now\n      Rails.logger.info(\"Startup: container sync completed\")\n    rescue => e\n      Rails.logger.warn(\"Startup: container sync failed: #{e.message}\")\n    end\n  end\nend",
  "new_string": "Rails.application.config.after_initialize do\n  # Only run in production/development, skip in test environment\n  # Skip if Docker socket is not available (e.g. migrate container)\n  unless Rails.env.test? || !File.exist?(\"/var/run/docker.sock\")\n    begin\n      # Run container sync to verify all sandboxes\n      # This will mark any missing containers as destroyed\n      ContainerSyncJob.perform_now\n      Rails.logger.info(\"Startup: container sync completed\")\n    rescue => e\n      Rails.logger.warn(\"Startup: container sync failed: #{e.message}\")\n    end\n  end\nend"
}
```

```
     1→class Sandbox < ApplicationRecord
     2→  include ActionView::RecordIdentifier  # For dom_id in Turbo broadcasts
     3→
     4→  belongs_to :user
     5→  has_many :routes, dependent: :destroy
     6→
     7→  SSH_PORT_RANGE = (2201..2299)
     8→  VNC_GEOMETRIES = %w[1280x900 1366x768 1440x900 1600x900 1920x1080 2560x1440].freeze
     9→  VNC_DEPTHS = [ 8, 16, 24, 32 ].freeze
    10→
    11→  validates :name, presence: true,
    12→    uniqueness: { scope: :user_id, conditions: -> { where.not(status: "destroyed") } },
    13→    format: { with: /\A[a-z][a-z0-9_-]{0,62}\z/, message: "must be lowercase alphanumeric" }
    14→  validates :ssh_port, presence: true,
    15→    uniqueness: { conditions: -> { where.not(status: "destroyed") } },
    16→    inclusion: { in: SSH_PORT_RANGE }
    17→  validates :status, inclusion: { in: %w[pending running stopped destroyed] }
    18→  validates :image, presence: true
    19→  validates :vnc_geometry, inclusion: { in: VNC_GEOMETRIES }
    20→  validates :vnc_depth, inclusion: { in: VNC_DEPTHS }
    21→
    22→  scope :active, -> { where.not(status: "destroyed") }
    23→  scope :running, -> { where(status: "running") }
    24→
    25→  before_validation :assign_ssh_port, on: :create
    26→
    27→  # Turbo Streams for real-time UI updates
    28→  after_create_commit :broadcast_prepend_to_dashboard
    29→  after_update_commit :broadcast_replace_to_dashboard
    30→  after_destroy_commit :broadcast_remove_from_dashboard
    31→
    32→  def full_name
    33→    "#{user.name}-#{name}"
    34→  end
    35→
    36→  def connect_command(host: ENV.fetch("SANDCASTLE_HOST", "localhost"))
    37→    "ssh -p #{ssh_port} #{user.name}@#{host}"
    38→  end
    39→
    40→  def routed?
    41→    routes.loaded? ? routes.any? : routes.exists?
    42→  end
    43→
    44→  def temp?
    45→    temporary?
    46→  end
    47→
    48→  # Job lifecycle management
    49→  def job_in_progress?
    50→    job_status.present?
    51→  end
    52→
    53→  def job_failed?
    54→    job_error.present?
    55→  end
    56→
    57→  def start_job(status)
    58→    update!(job_status: status, job_started_at: Time.current, job_error: nil)
    59→  end
    60→
    61→  def finish_job
    62→    update!(job_status: nil, job_started_at: nil)
    63→  end
    64→
    65→  def fail_job(error_message)
    66→    update!(job_status: nil, job_error: error_message, job_started_at: nil)
    67→  end
    68→
    69→  private
    70→
    71→  def broadcast_prepend_to_dashboard
    72→    broadcast_prepend_to(
    73→      [user, "dashboard"],
    74→      partial: "dashboard/sandbox",
    75→      locals: { sandbox: self },
    76→      target: "sandboxes"
    77→    )
    78→  end
    79→
    80→  def broadcast_replace_to_dashboard
    81→    # If sandbox is destroyed, remove it from the dashboard instead of replacing
    82→    if status == "destroyed"
    83→      broadcast_remove_to([user, "dashboard"], target: dom_id(self))
    84→    else
    85→      broadcast_replace_to(
    86→        [user, "dashboard"],
    87→        partial: "dashboard/sandbox",
    88→        locals: { sandbox: self },
    89→        target: dom_id(self)
    90→      )
    91→    end
    92→  end
    93→
    94→  def broadcast_remove_from_dashboard
    95→    broadcast_remove_to([user, "dashboard"], target: dom_id(self))
    96→  end
    97→
    98→  def assign_ssh_port
    99→    return if ssh_port.present?
   100→
   101→    used_ports = Sandbox.where.not(status: "destroyed").pluck(:ssh_port)
   102→    available = SSH_PORT_RANGE.to_a - used_ports
   103→    self.ssh_port = available.first || raise("No SSH ports available")
   104→  end
   105→end
   106→
```

> AGENT

Now I understand the architecture. The issue is a **race condition**: the `SandboxProvisionJob` completes (1582ms) before the browser can establish the WebSocket subscription. By the time `turbo_stream_from` subscribes, the cable messages are already in the past — Solid Cable's `from_time` is set to _now_ and all prior messages are ignored.

The fix: add a polling fallback for in-progress sandboxes. Let me check what Stimulus controllers already exist.

```
     1→class SandboxesController < ApplicationController
     2→  before_action :set_sandbox, only: [ :destroy, :start, :stop, :retry ]
     3→
     4→  def new
     5→    authorize Sandbox
     6→    @snapshots = SandboxManager.new.list_snapshots(user: Current.user)
     7→  end
     8→
     9→  def create
    10→    authorize Sandbox
    11→
    12→    image = if params[:snapshot].present?
    13→      "sc-snap-#{Current.user.name}:#{params[:snapshot]}"
    14→    else
    15→      params[:image].presence || SandboxManager::DEFAULT_IMAGE
    16→    end
    17→
    18→    # Build sandbox record
    19→    # Note: temporary sandboxes can only be created via CLI
    20→    sandbox = Current.user.sandboxes.build(
    21→      name: params.require(:name),
    22→      status: "pending",
    23→      image: image,
    24→      persistent_volume: params[:persistent] == "1",
    25→      mount_home: params[:mount_home] == "1",
    26→      data_path: params[:data_path].presence,
    27→      tailscale: params[:tailscale] == "1",
    28→      vnc_enabled: params[:vnc_enabled] != "0",
    29→      vnc_geometry: Sandbox::VNC_GEOMETRIES.include?(params[:vnc_geometry]) ? params[:vnc_geometry] : "1280x900",
    30→      vnc_depth: Sandbox::VNC_DEPTHS.include?(params[:vnc_depth].to_i) ? params[:vnc_depth].to_i : 24,
    31→      temporary: false
    32→    )
    33→
    34→    if sandbox.persistent_volume
    35→      sandbox.volume_path = "#{SandboxManager::DATA_DIR}/sandboxes/#{sandbox.full_name}/vol"
    36→    end
    37→
    38→    if sandbox.save
    39→      # Enqueue async job
    40→      SandboxProvisionJob.perform_later(sandbox_id: sandbox.id)
    41→
    42→      # Optimistic redirect - dashboard will update via Turbo
    43→      redirect_to root_path, notice: "Creating sandcastle #{sandbox.name}..."
    44→    else
    45→      @snapshots = SandboxManager.new.list_snapshots(user: Current.user)
    46→      flash.now[:alert] = "Failed to create sandbox: #{sandbox.errors.full_messages.join(', ')}"
    47→      render :new, status: :unprocessable_entity
    48→    end
    49→  rescue => e
    50→    @snapshots = SandboxManager.new.list_snapshots(user: Current.user)
    51→    flash.now[:alert] = "Failed to create sandbox: #{e.message}"
    52→    render :new, status: :unprocessable_entity
    53→  end
    54→
    55→  def destroy
    56→    if @sandbox.job_in_progress?
    57→      redirect_to root_path, alert: "Operation already in progress"
    58→      return
    59→    end
    60→
    61→    @sandbox.start_job("destroying")
    62→    SandboxDestroyJob.perform_later(sandbox_id: @sandbox.id)
    63→
    64→    respond_to do |format|
    65→      format.html { redirect_to root_path, notice: "Destroying sandcastle #{@sandbox.name}..." }
    66→      format.turbo_stream { render turbo_stream: turbo_stream.replace(@sandbox, partial: "dashboard/sandbox", locals: { sandbox: @sandbox }) }
    67→    end
    68→  end
    69→
    70→  def start
    71→    if @sandbox.job_in_progress?
    72→      redirect_to root_path, alert: "Operation already in progress"
    73→      return
    74→    end
    75→
    76→    @sandbox.start_job("starting")
    77→    SandboxStartJob.perform_later(sandbox_id: @sandbox.id)
    78→
    79→    respond_to do |format|
    80→      format.html { redirect_to root_path, notice: "Starting sandcastle #{@sandbox.name}..." }
    81→      format.turbo_stream { render turbo_stream: turbo_stream.replace(@sandbox, partial: "dashboard/sandbox", locals: { sandbox: @sandbox }) }
    82→    end
    83→  end
    84→
    85→  def stop
    86→    if @sandbox.job_in_progress?
    87→      redirect_to root_path, alert: "Operation already in progress"
    88→      return
    89→    end
    90→
    91→    @sandbox.start_job("stopping")
    92→    SandboxStopJob.perform_later(sandbox_id: @sandbox.id)
    93→
    94→    respond_to do |format|
    95→      format.html { redirect_to root_path, notice: "Stopping sandcastle #{@sandbox.name}..." }
    96→      format.turbo_stream { render turbo_stream: turbo_stream.replace(@sandbox, partial: "dashboard/sandbox", locals: { sandbox: @sandbox }) }
    97→    end
    98→  end
    99→
   100→  def retry
   101→    return unless @sandbox.job_failed?
   102→
   103→    @sandbox.update!(job_error: nil)
   104→
   105→    case @sandbox.status
   106→    when "destroyed", "pending"
   107→      redirect_to root_path, alert: "Cannot retry creation. Please create a new sandbox."
   108→    when "stopped"
   109→      SandboxStartJob.perform_later(sandbox_id: @sandbox.id)
   110→      redirect_to root_path, notice: "Retrying start..."
   111→    when "running"
   112→      redirect_to root_path, alert: "Sandbox is already running"
   113→    end
   114→  end
   115→
   116→  private
   117→
   118→  def set_sandbox
   119→    @sandbox = policy_scope(Sandbox).find(params[:id])
   120→    authorize @sandbox
   121→  end
   122→end
   123→
```

```
     1→class DashboardController < ApplicationController
     2→  def index
     3→    @sandboxes = policy_scope(Sandbox).includes(:user, :routes).order(:name)
     4→  end
     5→
     6→  def stats
     7→    sandbox = policy_scope(Sandbox).find(params[:id])
     8→    authorize sandbox, :stats?
     9→
    10→    if sandbox.status == "running" && sandbox.container_id.present?
    11→      container = Docker::Container.get(sandbox.container_id)
    12→      raw = container.stats(stream: false)
    13→
    14→      # Handle case where container is shutting down and returns nil/incomplete stats
    15→      if raw.present?
    16→        networks = raw["networks"] || {}
    17→        net_rx = networks.values.sum { |n| n["rx_bytes"] || 0 }
    18→        net_tx = networks.values.sum { |n| n["tx_bytes"] || 0 }
    19→
    20→        blkio = raw.dig("blkio_stats", "io_service_bytes_recursive") || []
    21→        disk_read = blkio.select { |e| e["op"]&.downcase == "read" }.sum { |e| e["value"] || 0 }
    22→        disk_write = blkio.select { |e| e["op"]&.downcase == "write" }.sum { |e| e["value"] || 0 }
    23→
    24→        @stats = {
    25→          cpu_percent: calculate_cpu_percent(raw),
    26→          memory_mb: (raw.dig("memory_stats", "usage") || 0) / 1_048_576.0,
    27→          memory_limit_mb: (raw.dig("memory_stats", "limit") || 0) / 1_048_576.0,
    28→          net_rx: net_rx,
    29→          net_tx: net_tx,
    30→          disk_read: disk_read,
    31→          disk_write: disk_write,
    32→          pids: raw.dig("pids_stats", "current") || 0
    33→        }
    34→      end
    35→    end
    36→
    37→    render partial: "sandbox_stats", locals: { stats: @stats, sandbox: sandbox }
    38→  rescue ActiveRecord::RecordNotFound
    39→    render partial: "sandbox_stats", locals: { stats: nil, sandbox: nil }
    40→  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError
    41→    render partial: "sandbox_stats", locals: { stats: nil, sandbox: sandbox }
    42→  end
    43→
    44→  private
    45→
    46→  def calculate_cpu_percent(stats)
    47→    cpu_delta = stats.dig("cpu_stats", "cpu_usage", "total_usage").to_f -
    48→                stats.dig("precpu_stats", "cpu_usage", "total_usage").to_f
    49→    system_delta = stats.dig("cpu_stats", "system_cpu_usage").to_f -
    50→                   stats.dig("precpu_stats", "system_cpu_usage").to_f
    51→    num_cpus = stats.dig("cpu_stats", "online_cpus") || 1
    52→
    53→    return 0.0 if system_delta.zero?
    54→    ((cpu_delta / system_delta) * num_cpus * 100.0).round(1)
    55→  end
    56→end
    57→
```

> AGENT

Now I have a complete picture. The root cause is a **race condition**: `SandboxProvisionJob` (1582ms) can complete before the browser establishes its ActionCable WebSocket subscription. Solid Cable's `from_time` is set at subscription time, so all broadcasts that fired before the subscription was established are missed.

The fix: add a polling fallback for in-progress sandboxes. Here's what I'll implement:
1. A `card` route/action that returns the sandbox partial as a Turbo Stream replace
2. A Stimulus controller that polls every 3s when a sandbox job is in-progress
3. Wire the Stimulus controller to sandbox cards when `job_in_progress?`

Let me implement all changes:

```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/app/javascript/controllers/sandbox_polling_controller.js",
  "content": "import { Controller } from \"@hotwired/stimulus\"\n\n// Polls the server for the latest sandbox card HTML when a job is in progress.\n// Provides a fallback for cases where the Turbo Stream broadcast is missed\n// (e.g. WebSocket not yet connected when the job completes).\nexport default class extends Controller {\n  static values = { url: String }\n\n  connect() {\n    this.timer = setInterval(() => this.refresh(), 3000)\n  }\n\n  disconnect() {\n    clearInterval(this.timer)\n  }\n\n  refresh() {\n    fetch(this.urlValue, {\n      headers: {\n        \"Accept\": \"text/vnd.turbo-stream.html\",\n        \"X-CSRF-Token\": document.querySelector(\"meta[name='csrf-token']\")?.content\n      }\n    })\n      .then(r => r.text())\n      .then(html => Turbo.renderStreamMessage(html))\n      .catch(() => {})\n  }\n}\n"
}
```

> AGENT

The deploy completed successfully. The stack is running at `http://localhost:8080` and `https://localhost:8443`.

Here's a summary of what was fixed and why:

---

## Root cause: Race condition with Solid Cable

The `SandboxProvisionJob` completes in ~1.5 seconds. During that time the browser is:
1. Following the redirect to the dashboard
2. Loading HTML/CSS/JS
3. Establishing the ActionCable WebSocket

Solid Cable tracks a `from_time` per subscription — messages written **before** the subscription connects are not replayed. So if the job finishes before the WebSocket is ready, all broadcasts are silently missed.

## Fix: Polling fallback for in-progress sandboxes

**5 changes across 5 files:**

| File | Change |
|------|--------|
| `config/routes.rb` | Added `GET /sandboxes/:id/card` route |
| `app/controllers/dashboard_controller.rb` | Added `card` action — returns the sandbox partial as a Turbo Stream replace |
| `app/policies/sandbox_policy.rb` | Added `card?` authorization method |
| `app/javascript/controllers/sandbox_polling_controller.js` | New Stimulus controller that polls every 3s |
| `app/views/dashboard/_sandbox.html.erb` | Attaches the Stimulus controller when `job_in_progress?` |

**How it works:** When a sandbox card renders in "Creating" (or any in-progress) state, the Stimulus `sandbox-polling` controller attaches and polls `/sandboxes/:id/card` every 3 seconds. The response is a Turbo Stream replace with the freshly-rendered partial. When the job finishes, the replaced card won't have `data-controller="sandbox-polling"`, so Stimulus disconnects the controller and polling stops automatically.

     1→# VNC Debug Session - 2026-02-15
     2→
     3→## Issues Found & Fixed
     4→
     5→### 1. Port Mismatch in VncManager ✅ FIXED
     6→
     7→**Problem:** VncManager was trying to connect to noVNC container on port 8080, but noVNC actually listens on port 6080.
     8→
     9→**Fix:** Updated `app/services/vnc_manager.rb:223`
    10→
    11→```ruby
    12→# Before:
    13→"servers" => [ { "url" => "http://#{container_name}:8080" } ]
    14→
    15→# After:
    16→"servers" => [ { "url" => "http://#{container_name}:6080" } ]
    17→```
    18→
    19→**Also updated:** Existing Traefik config `/data/traefik/dynamic/vnc-38.yml` to use port 6080.
    20→
    21→### 2. Missing VNC Server in Sandbox Container ⏳ PENDING
    22→
    23→**Problem:** Running sandbox containers use old image (`ghcr.io/thieso2/sandcastle-sandbox:latest`) that doesn't have VNC tools (Xvfb, x11vnc) installed.
    24→
    25→**Root Cause:** The sandbox Dockerfile includes VNC installation, but deployed containers are using an older image version built before VNC support was added.
    26→
    27→**Fix Applied:** Updated `images/sandbox/Dockerfile` to install VNC tools:
    28→
    29→```dockerfile
    30→# GUI tools: Xvfb (virtual X server) and x11vnc for browser access
    31→RUN apt-get update && apt-get install -y \
    32→    xvfb x11vnc xfonts-base xfonts-100dpi xfonts-75dpi \
    33→    xterm fluxbox \
    34→    && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*
    35→```
    36→
    37→**Note:** Chrome installation commented out due to dependency conflicts with Ubuntu 25.10.
    38→
    39→### 3. Traefik Configuration ✅ WORKING
    40→
    41→**Status:** Traefik dynamic config exists and is properly configured. The VNC routing works correctly after the port fix.
    42→
    43→## What Works
    44→
    45→- ✅ noVNC container runs and listens on port 6080
    46→- ✅ Traefik routing configured with forwardAuth middleware
    47→- ✅ Authentication flow (`VncController#auth`) works correctly
    48→- ✅ VncManager service logic is sound (after port fix)
    49→- ✅ Web container restarted to apply code changes
    50→
    51→## What's Missing
    52→
    53→- ⏳ VNC server (Xvfb + x11vnc) running in sandbox containers
    54→- ⏳ Rebuild sandbox image with VNC tools
    55→- ⏳ Recreate sandbox with new image
    56→
    57→## Next Steps to Enable VNC
    58→
    59→### 1. Rebuild Sandbox Image
    60→
    61→```bash
    62→cd images/sandbox
    63→docker build -t sandcastle-sandbox:latest .
    64→```
    65→
    66→**Note:** This may take 10-15 minutes due to Docker installation step. Network speed affects build time.
    67→
    68→### 2. Verify VNC Tools in Image
    69→
    70→```bash
    71→docker run --rm sandcastle-sandbox:latest bash -c \
    72→  'command -v Xvfb && command -v x11vnc && echo "✓ VNC tools installed"'
    73→```
    74→
    75→Should output:
    76→```
    77→/usr/bin/Xvfb
    78→/usr/bin/x11vnc
    79→✓ VNC tools installed
    80→```
    81→
    82→### 3. Recreate Sandbox with New Image
    83→
    84→Via Rails console:
    85→
    86→```bash
    87→docker exec -it sandcastle-web ./bin/rails runner "
    88→  sandbox = Sandbox.find_by(name: 'quantum-viper')
    89→  if sandbox
    90→    user = sandbox.user
    91→    persistent = sandbox.persistent_volume
    92→
    93→    # Destroy old sandbox
    94→    SandboxManager.new.destroy(sandbox: sandbox)
    95→
    96→    # Create new sandbox with updated image
    97→    SandboxManager.new.create(
    98→      user: user,
    99→      name: 'quantum-viper',
   100→      image: 'sandcastle-sandbox:latest',
   101→      persistent: persistent
   102→    )
   103→  end
   104→"
   105→```
   106→
   107→Or via web UI:
   108→1. Delete the sandbox
   109→2. Create a new one with the same name
   110→
   111→### 4. Test VNC Connection
   112→
   113→1. Click the browser icon for the sandbox in the web UI
   114→2. Wait for the "Connecting to browser..." page
   115→3. Should redirect to noVNC interface showing the desktop
   116→
   117→## Technical Details
   118→
   119→### VNC Architecture
   120→
   121→```
   122→Browser
   123→  ↓ HTTPS
   124→Traefik (:8443)
   125→  ↓ forwardAuth → VncController#auth (validates session)
   126→  ↓ stripPrefix (/vnc/{id}/novnc)
   127→noVNC Container (port 6080)
   128→  ↓ VNC protocol (port 5900)
   129→Sandbox Container
   130→  ├─ Xvfb :99 (virtual X display)
   131→  └─ x11vnc -display :99 -rfbport 5900
   132→```
   133→
   134→### Container Names
   135→
   136→- noVNC: `sc-vnc-{user}-{sandbox}` (e.g., `sc-vnc-thies-quantum-viper`)
   137→- Sandbox: `{user}-{sandbox}` (e.g., `thies-quantum-viper`)
   138→- Network: `sandcastle-web` (bridge)
   139→
   140→### VNC Server Startup
   141→
   142→The sandbox entrypoint (`images/sandbox/entrypoint.sh:71-80`) automatically starts:
   143→
   144→1. **Xvfb** on display :99 with 1920x1080 resolution
   145→2. **x11vnc** listening on port 5900, connected to display :99
   146→
   147→```bash
   148→Xvfb :99 -screen 0 1920x1080x24 &>/var/log/xvfb.log &
   149→sleep 1
   150→DISPLAY=:99 x11vnc -shared -forever -nopw -rfbport 5900 &>/var/log/x11vnc.log &
   151→```
   152→
   153→### Debugging Commands
   154→
   155→```bash
   156→# Check if noVNC container is running
   157→docker ps | grep sc-vnc
   158→
   159→# Check noVNC logs
   160→docker logs sc-vnc-thies-quantum-viper
   161→
   162→# Check if VNC server is running in sandbox
   163→docker exec thies-quantum-viper ps aux | grep -E "Xvfb|x11vnc"
   164→
   165→# Check VNC server logs
   166→docker exec thies-quantum-viper cat /var/log/xvfb.log
   167→docker exec thies-quantum-viper cat /var/log/x11vnc.log
   168→
   169→# Test VNC connection from sandbox to itself
   170→docker exec thies-quantum-viper nc -zv localhost 5900
   171→
   172→# Check Traefik config
   173→docker exec sandcastle-web cat /data/traefik/dynamic/vnc-38.yml
   174→
   175→# Check noVNC is listening on port 6080
   176→docker exec sc-vnc-thies-quantum-viper netstat -tlnp | grep 6080
   177→```
   178→
   179→## Files Modified
   180→
   181→1. `app/services/vnc_manager.rb` - Port fix (8080 → 6080)
   182→2. `images/sandbox/Dockerfile` - Added VNC tools installation
   183→3. `/data/traefik/dynamic/vnc-38.yml` - Runtime config update (port fix)
   184→
   185→## Known Issues
   186→
   187→### Ubuntu 25.10 + Chrome Compatibility
   188→
   189→Chrome installation fails on Ubuntu 25.10 due to missing dependencies:
   190→- `libatspi2.0-0:amd64`
   191→- `libxdamage1:amd64`
   192→- `libxext6:amd64`
   193→- etc.
   194→
   195→**Workaround:** Chrome installation commented out in Dockerfile. Users can install it manually if needed, or we can switch to Ubuntu 24.04 LTS for better package compatibility.
   196→
   197→### Slow Package Downloads
   198→
   199→During testing, apt-get package downloads were extremely slow (20+ minutes for a few packages). This affected both image builds and in-container installations.
   200→
   201→**Recommendation:** Run builds during better network conditions or consider using Ubuntu package mirrors closer to your location.
   202→
   203→## Testing Checklist
   204→
   205→Once sandbox is recreated with new image:
   206→
   207→- [ ] VNC button in web UI triggers `/sandboxes/{id}/vnc` POST
   208→- [ ] Redirects to `/sandboxes/{id}/vnc/wait` waiting page
   209→- [ ] Status polling (`/sandboxes/{id}/vnc/status`) returns "ready"
   210→- [ ] Redirects to `/vnc/{id}/novnc` URL
   211→- [ ] Traefik forwardAuth validates session
   212→- [ ] noVNC interface loads in browser
   213→- [ ] Can see Xvfb desktop (black screen is normal - no apps running)
   214→- [ ] Can interact with desktop (right-click, open xterm, etc.)
   215→
   216→## Additional Notes
   217→
   218→- Web container was restarted to pick up VncManager code changes
   219→- Existing VNC session for sandbox #38 should be closed and reopened after sandbox recreation
   220→- The web container restart means the code fix is already live in the local environment
   221→

```
     1→class VncController < ApplicationController
     2→  allow_unauthenticated_access only: :auth
     3→  skip_before_action :require_password_change, only: :auth
     4→  skip_forgery_protection only: :open
     5→
     6→  def open
     7→    sandbox = find_sandbox
     8→
     9→    VncManager.new.open(sandbox: sandbox)
    10→    redirect_to vnc_wait_sandbox_path(sandbox), status: :see_other
    11→  rescue VncManager::Error => e

    14→
    15→  def wait

    17→    id = @sandbox.id

    21→  def status
```
