    23→    ready = VncManager.new.active?(sandbox: sandbox)
    24→    render json: { status: ready ? "ready" : "waiting" }
    25→  end
    26→
    27→  def close
    28→    sandbox = find_sandbox
    29→
    30→    VncManager.new.close(sandbox: sandbox)
    31→    redirect_to root_path, notice: "Browser session closed"
    32→  rescue VncManager::Error => e
    33→    redirect_to root_path, alert: e.message
    34→  end
    35→
    36→  private
    37→
    38→  def find_sandbox
    39→    if Current.user.admin?
    40→      Sandbox.active.find(params[:id])
    41→    else
    42→      Current.user.sandboxes.active.find(params[:id])
    43→    end
    44→  end
    45→
    46→  # Build the full VNC URL. In production, Traefik is the entry point
    47→  # so a relative path works. In local dev (selfsigned TLS), Rails may be
    48→  # accessed directly on a different port, so we need an absolute URL.
    49→  def vnc_redirect_url(path)
    50→    base = ENV["SANDCASTLE_VNC_URL"] || ENV["SANDCASTLE_TERMINAL_URL"]
    51→    base ? "#{base}#{path}" : path
    52→  end
    53→
    54→  public
    55→
    56→  # Called by Traefik forwardAuth. Returns 200 to allow, or redirects to
    57→  # login (which Traefik passes through to the browser).
    58→  def auth
    59→    forwarded_uri = request.headers["X-Forwarded-Uri"] || ""
    60→    match = forwarded_uri.match(%r{/vnc/(\d+)})
    61→    head(:unauthorized) and return unless match
    62→
    63→    session_record = find_session_by_cookie
    64→    unless session_record
    65→      # Build the original VNC URL from Traefik's forwarded headers
    66→      # so the user returns here after logging in.
    67→      proto = request.headers["X-Forwarded-Proto"] || "https"
    68→      host  = request.headers["X-Forwarded-Host"] || request.host_with_port
    69→      original_url = "#{proto}://#{host}#{forwarded_uri}"
    70→      session[:return_to_after_authenticating] = original_url
    71→      redirect_to new_session_url, allow_other_host: true
    72→      return
    73→    end
    74→
    75→    user = session_record.user
    76→    sandbox = Sandbox.active.find_by(id: match[1].to_i)
    77→    head(:unauthorized) and return unless sandbox && (sandbox.user_id == user.id || user.admin?)
    78→
    79→    head :ok
    80→  end
    81→end
    82→

     1→class VncManager
     2→  DATA_DIR = ENV.fetch("SANDCASTLE_DATA_DIR", "/data")
     3→  NOVNC_IMAGE = ENV.fetch("SANDCASTLE_NOVNC_IMAGE", "gotget/novnc:latest")
     4→  NETWORK_NAME = "sandcastle-web"
     5→  DYNAMIC_DIR = File.join(DATA_DIR, "traefik", "dynamic")
     6→
     7→  class Error < StandardError; end
     8→
     9→  # Opens a web-based VNC session for the given sandbox.
    10→  # Returns the URL path to the noVNC session.
    11→  def open(sandbox:)
    12→    raise Error, "Sandbox is not running" unless sandbox.status == "running"
    13→    raise Error, "Sandbox has no container" if sandbox.container_id.blank?
    14→
    15→    user = sandbox.user
    16→    container_name = vnc_container_name(sandbox)
    17→
    18→    # Idempotent: if noVNC container already running, return URL
    19→    if container_running?(container_name)
    20→      return vnc_url(sandbox)
    21→    end
    22→
    23→    pull_image
    24→    ensure_network
    25→    connect_sandbox_to_network(sandbox)
    26→
    27→    # Write Traefik config early so it has time to detect the new route
    28→    write_traefik_config(sandbox)
    29→
    30→    create_novnc_container(sandbox: sandbox, user: user)
    31→
    32→    # Give Traefik time to detect the new route configuration
    33→    sleep 2
    34→
    35→    vnc_url(sandbox)
    36→  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError => e
    37→    raise Error, "Failed to open browser: #{e.message}"
    38→  rescue SystemCallError => e
    39→    raise Error, "Failed to open browser: #{e.message}"
    40→  end
    41→
    42→  # Closes the web VNC session for the given sandbox.
    43→  def close(sandbox:)
    44→    container_name = vnc_container_name(sandbox)
    45→
    46→    # Stop and remove noVNC container
    47→    begin
    48→      container = Docker::Container.get(container_name)
    49→      container.stop(t: 3) rescue nil
    50→      container.delete(force: true)
    51→    rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
    52→      # Already gone
    53→    end
    54→
    55→    # Delete Traefik config
    56→    delete_traefik_config(sandbox)
    57→  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError => e
    58→    Rails.logger.error("VncManager: close failed for #{sandbox.full_name}: #{e.message}")
    59→  end
    60→
    61→  # Returns true if the noVNC container is running for this sandbox.
    62→  def active?(sandbox:)
    63→    container_running?(vnc_container_name(sandbox))
    64→  end
    65→
    66→  # Removes orphaned noVNC containers whose sandbox no longer exists or is not running.
    67→  def cleanup_orphaned
    68→    Docker::Container.all(all: true).each do |container|
    69→      name = container.info.dig("Names")&.first&.delete_prefix("/")
    70→      next unless name&.start_with?("sc-vnc-")
    71→
    72→      labels = container.info["Labels"] || {}
    73→      sandbox_id = labels["sandcastle.sandbox_id"]&.to_i
    74→
    75→      sandbox = sandbox_id ? Sandbox.find_by(id: sandbox_id) : nil
    76→      should_remove = sandbox.nil? || sandbox.status != "running"
    77→
    78→      if should_remove
    79→        container.stop(t: 3) rescue nil
    80→        container.delete(force: true)
    81→        Rails.logger.info("VncManager: removed orphaned noVNC container #{name}")
    82→
    83→        # Clean up Traefik config if we have a sandbox_id
    84→        if sandbox_id
    85→          config_path = File.join(DYNAMIC_DIR, "vnc-#{sandbox_id}.yml")
    86→          File.delete(config_path) if File.exist?(config_path)
    87→        end
    88→      end
    89→    end
    90→  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError => e
    91→    Rails.logger.error("VncManager: orphan cleanup failed: #{e.message}")
    92→  end
    93→
    94→  private
    95→
    96→  def vnc_container_name(sandbox)
    97→    "sc-vnc-#{sandbox.full_name}"
    98→  end
    99→
   100→  def vnc_url(sandbox)
   101→    id = sandbox.id
   102→    # Strip prefix is /vnc/{id}, so all requests under /vnc/{id}/* are forwarded
   103→    # to the noVNC container with the prefix removed. This keeps noVNC's relative
   104→    # asset paths (package.json, core/*.js) resolving correctly within the prefix.
   105→    # The ?path= tells noVNC where to connect the WebSocket; Traefik strips
   106→    # /vnc/{id} leaving /websockify which websockify handles.
   107→    "/vnc/#{id}/vnc.html?path=vnc/#{id}/websockify"
   108→  end
   109→
   110→  def container_running?(name)
   111→    container = Docker::Container.get(name)
   112→    container.json.dig("State", "Running") == true
   113→  rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
   114→    false
   115→  end
   116→
   117→  def pull_image
   118→    Docker::Image.get(NOVNC_IMAGE)
   119→  rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
   120→    Docker::Image.create("fromImage" => NOVNC_IMAGE)
   121→  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError
   122→    raise Error, "Failed to pull #{NOVNC_IMAGE} — check network connectivity"
   123→  end
   124→
   125→  def ensure_network
   126→    Docker::Network.get(NETWORK_NAME)
   127→  rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
   128→    Docker::Network.create(NETWORK_NAME, "Driver" => "bridge")
   129→  end
   130→
   131→  def connect_sandbox_to_network(sandbox)
   132→    return unless sandbox.container_id.present?
   133→
   134→    network = Docker::Network.get(NETWORK_NAME)
   135→    container = Docker::Container.get(sandbox.container_id)
   136→
   137→    networks = container.json.dig("NetworkSettings", "Networks") || {}
   138→    return if networks.key?(NETWORK_NAME)
   139→
   140→    network.connect(sandbox.container_id)
   141→  rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
   142→    # Container no longer exists - sync job will fix the DB state
   143→    raise Error, "Sandbox container not found. Please refresh and try again."
   144→  end
   145→
   146→  def create_novnc_container(sandbox:, user:)
   147→    container_name = vnc_container_name(sandbox)
   148→
   149→    # Remove any existing container with this name
   150→    begin
   151→      old = Docker::Container.get(container_name)
   152→      old.stop(t: 3) rescue nil
   153→      old.delete(force: true)
   154→    rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
   155→      # No existing container
   156→    end
   157→
   158→    host_config = {
   159→      "NetworkMode" => NETWORK_NAME,
   160→      "RestartPolicy" => { "Name" => "no" },
   161→      "Memory" => 128 * 1024 * 1024, # 128MB — noVNC static files + websockify proxy
   162→      "NanoCpus" => 250_000_000 # 0.25 CPU
   163→    }
   164→
   165→    # Run the noVNC image using its built-in launch.sh, which starts both the
   166→    # mini-webserver (noVNC HTML/JS) and websockify (WS→RFB proxy) on port 6080.
   167→    # Traefik routes everything under /vnc/{id} to this container; after
   168→    # stripPrefix the websockify path becomes /websockify as noVNC expects.
   169→    container = Docker::Container.create(
   170→      "name" => container_name,
   171→      "Image" => NOVNC_IMAGE,
   172→      "Cmd" => [ "--vnc", "#{sandbox.full_name}:5900", "--listen", "6080" ],
   173→      "HostConfig" => host_config,
   174→      "Labels" => {
   175→        "sandcastle.sandbox_id" => sandbox.id.to_s,
   176→        "sandcastle.role" => "novnc"
   177→      }
   178→    )
   179→
   180→    container.start
   181→    container
   182→  end
   183→
   184→  def write_traefik_config(sandbox)
   185→    FileUtils.mkdir_p(DYNAMIC_DIR)
   186→
   187→    host = ENV.fetch("SANDCASTLE_HOST", "localhost")
   188→    id = sandbox.id
   189→    container_name = vnc_container_name(sandbox)
   190→    sandbox_name   = sandbox.full_name
   191→
   192→    base_rule = if ENV["SANDCASTLE_TLS_MODE"] == "selfsigned"
   193→      "HostRegexp(`.+`) && PathPrefix(`/vnc/#{id}`)"
   194→    else
   195→      "Host(`#{host}`) && PathPrefix(`/vnc/#{id}`)"
   196→    end
   197→
   198→    # Single router: all traffic under /vnc/{id} goes to the noVNC sidecar.
   199→    # The noVNC container (gotget/novnc launch.sh) serves static HTML/JS on /
   200→    # and proxies WebSocket VNC frames via websockify on /websockify, both on
   201→    # port 6080. stripPrefix removes /vnc/{id} before forwarding, so noVNC's
   202→    # relative asset paths (package.json, core/*.js, /websockify) resolve correctly.
   203→    config = {
   204→      "http" => {
   205→        "routers" => {
   206→          "vnc-#{id}" => {
   207→            "rule" => base_rule,
   208→            "service" => "vnc-#{id}",
   209→            "entryPoints" => [ "websecure" ],
   210→            "tls" => tls_config,
   211→            "middlewares" => [ "vnc-auth-#{id}", "vnc-stripprefix-#{id}" ],
   212→            "priority" => 100
   213→          }
   214→        },
   215→        "middlewares" => {
   216→          "vnc-auth-#{id}" => {
   217→            "forwardAuth" => {
   218→              "address" => "http://sandcastle-web:80/vnc/auth",
   219→              "trustForwardHeader" => true
   220→            }
   221→          },
   222→          "vnc-stripprefix-#{id}" => {
   223→            "stripPrefix" => {
   224→              "prefixes" => [ "/vnc/#{id}" ]
   225→            }
   226→          }
   227→        },
   228→        "services" => {
   229→          "vnc-#{id}" => {
   230→            "loadBalancer" => {
   231→              "servers" => [ { "url" => "http://#{container_name}:6080" } ]
   232→            }
   233→          }
   234→        }
   235→      }
   236→    }
   237→
   238→    File.write(File.join(DYNAMIC_DIR, "vnc-#{id}.yml"), config.to_yaml)
   239→  end
   240→
   241→  def tls_config
   242→    if ENV["SANDCASTLE_TLS_MODE"] == "selfsigned"
   243→      {}
   244→    else
   245→      { "certResolver" => "letsencrypt" }
   246→    end
   247→  end
   248→
   249→  def delete_traefik_config(sandbox)
   250→    path = File.join(DYNAMIC_DIR, "vnc-#{sandbox.id}.yml")
   251→    File.delete(path) if File.exist?(path)
   252→  end
   253→end
   254→

```
     1→#!/bin/bash
     2→set -e
     3→
     4→USERNAME="${SANDCASTLE_USER:-sandbox}"
     5→SSH_KEY="${SANDCASTLE_SSH_KEY:-}"
     6→
     7→# Create user if not exists
     8→if ! id "$USERNAME" &>/dev/null; then
     9→    useradd -m -s /bin/bash -G sudo,docker "$USERNAME"
    10→    echo "$USERNAME ALL=(ALL) NOPASSWD:ALL" >> /etc/sudoers.d/sandcastle
    11→    chmod 0440 /etc/sudoers.d/sandcastle
    12→fi
    13→
    14→# Set up SSH authorized keys (append if not already present, preserving
    15→# any WeTTY keys that may have been injected for other sandboxes sharing
    16→# this user's home directory via bind mount).
    17→if [ -n "$SSH_KEY" ]; then
    18→    SSH_DIR="/home/$USERNAME/.ssh"
    19→    mkdir -p "$SSH_DIR"
    20→    if [ -f "$SSH_DIR/authorized_keys" ]; then
    21→        grep -qF "$SSH_KEY" "$SSH_DIR/authorized_keys" || echo "$SSH_KEY" >> "$SSH_DIR/authorized_keys"
    22→    else
    23→        echo "$SSH_KEY" > "$SSH_DIR/authorized_keys"
    24→    fi
    25→    chmod 700 "$SSH_DIR"
    26→    chmod 600 "$SSH_DIR/authorized_keys"
    27→    chown -R "$USERNAME:$USERNAME" "$SSH_DIR"
    28→fi
    29→
    30→# Ensure home directory ownership and permissions.
    31→# The host may create bind-mounted home dirs with 777 so Sysbox-mapped root
    32→# can write. Tighten to 755 here so sshd StrictModes is satisfied.
    33→chown "$USERNAME:$USERNAME" "/home/$USERNAME"
    34→chmod 755 "/home/$USERNAME"
    35→
    36→# Ensure workspace is accessible
    37→chown "$USERNAME:$USERNAME" /workspace 2>/dev/null || true
    38→
    39→# Seed mise + Claude Code into user's ~/.local/bin on first boot
    40→USER_LOCAL_BIN="/home/$USERNAME/.local/bin"
    41→mkdir -p "$USER_LOCAL_BIN"
    42→for tool in mise claude; do
    43→    if [ ! -f "$USER_LOCAL_BIN/$tool" ] && [ -f "/opt/sandcastle/bin/$tool" ]; then
    44→        cp "/opt/sandcastle/bin/$tool" "$USER_LOCAL_BIN/$tool"
    45→    fi
    46→done
    47→chown -R "$USERNAME:$USERNAME" "/home/$USERNAME/.local"
    48→
    49→# Configure git identity system-wide if provided
    50→if [ -n "$USER_FULLNAME" ] || [ -n "$USER_EMAIL" ]; then
    51→    {
    52→        echo "[user]"
    53→        [ -n "$USER_FULLNAME" ] && echo "    name = $USER_FULLNAME"
    54→        [ -n "$USER_EMAIL" ] && echo "    email = $USER_EMAIL"
    55→    } >> /etc/gitconfig
    56→fi
    57→
    58→# Generate SSH host keys if missing
    59→ssh-keygen -A
    60→
    61→# Resize /dev/shm to 2GB for Chrome. Docker's ShmSize HostConfig key is not
    62→# supported by sysbox-runc, so we do it here instead. The runtime bind-mounts
    63→# /dev/shm from a small constrained shm, so "remount,size=" fails; unmounting
    64→# it and mounting a fresh tmpfs works.
    65→umount /dev/shm 2>/dev/null || true
    66→mount -t tmpfs -o size=2g,mode=1777 tmpfs /dev/shm 2>/dev/null || true
    67→
    68→# Start Docker daemon in background (requires Sysbox runtime for isolated /var/lib/docker)
    69→# Don't wait for it to be ready - users can check with `docker info` after SSH login
    70→if command -v dockerd &>/dev/null && [ -e /dev/fuse ]; then
    71→    # Match inner Docker bridge MTU to container's eth0 to avoid packet fragmentation
    72→    ETH0_MTU=$(ip link show eth0 2>/dev/null | grep -oP 'mtu \K[0-9]+' || echo 1500)
    73→    dockerd --storage-driver=overlay2 --mtu="$ETH0_MTU" &>/var/log/dockerd.log &
    74→else
    75→    echo "Note: Docker-in-Docker not available (requires sysbox-runc runtime)" >&2
    76→fi
    77→
    78→# Start virtual X + VNC server for browser access.
    79→# Xvnc (TigerVNC) combines Xvfb and a VNC server in a single process and sends
    80→# the RFB banner immediately on connect — required for websockify compatibility.
    81→# x11vnc 0.9.17+ waits for client data before sending the banner, deadlocking
    82→# with websockify which also waits for the server to speak first.
    83→# Both processes run as $USERNAME (not root) for proper display ownership.
    84→VNC_ENABLED="${SANDCASTLE_VNC_ENABLED:-1}"
    85→VNC_GEOMETRY="${SANDCASTLE_VNC_GEOMETRY:-1280x900}"
    86→VNC_DEPTH="${SANDCASTLE_VNC_DEPTH:-24}"
    87→
    88→if command -v Xvnc &>/dev/null && [ "$VNC_ENABLED" = "1" ]; then
    89→    touch /var/log/xvnc.log /var/log/openbox.log
    90→    chown "$USERNAME:$USERNAME" /var/log/xvnc.log /var/log/openbox.log
    91→    su -s /bin/bash "$USERNAME" -c \
    92→        "Xvnc :99 -rfbport 5900 -SecurityTypes None -AlwaysShared -geometry ${VNC_GEOMETRY} -depth ${VNC_DEPTH} &>/var/log/xvnc.log &"
    93→    # Start Openbox window manager once the display is ready
    94→    if command -v openbox &>/dev/null; then
    95→        su -s /bin/bash "$USERNAME" -c \
    96→            'DISPLAY=:99 openbox &>/var/log/openbox.log &'
    97→    fi
    98→fi
    99→
   100→# Start SSH daemon in foreground
   101→exec /usr/sbin/sshd -D -e
   102→

    46→       -o /usr/bin/runc \

    48→
    49→# Docker Compose plugin

    53→    && chmod +x /usr/local/lib/docker/cli-plugins/docker-compose
    54→

    56→RUN mkdir -p /opt/sandcastle/bin \
    57→    && curl https://mise.run | REDACTED sh \
    58→    && curl -fsSL https://claude.ai/install.sh | bash \
    59→    && cp -L /root/.local/bin/claude /opt/sandcastle/bin/claude \
    60→    && rm -rf /root/.local/share/claude /root/.local/bin/claude
    61→
    62→# Entire CLI — git observability for AI agent sessions (https://entire.io)

    67→

> AGENT

noVNC is healthy from inside the network. The Bad Gateway was a **startup race condition**: the wait page checks `active?` which only verifies the Docker container is running, not that port 6080 is actually serving. Let me fix `active?` to include a TCP health check:

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_014PyUs83e4zXt2oM1YynP5K
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,

> AGENT

Web container is up. Now **try the VNC again** — click the "Browser" button in the dashboard for the zen-puma sandbox. This time:

1. The wait page will poll `active?`, which now does a TCP health check on port 6080 before declaring "ready"
2. The browser will only get redirected once noVNC is actually serving HTTP — no more premature Bad Gateway

There's one remaining thing to note: **the Xvnc you're running is temporary** (manually started in the old image container). When you create a new sandbox, the new sandbox image needs to be built with TigerVNC. That requires:

```bash
# Build sandbox image locally (takes ~10-15 min due to Docker/Chrome installs)
docker build -t ghcr.io/thieso2/sandcastle-sandbox:latest images/sandbox/
```

Then destroy and recreate the zen-puma sandbox, and the entrypoint will auto-start Xvnc on boot (the entrypoint.sh already handles this via `if command -v Xvnc &>/dev/null && [ "$VNC_ENABLED" = "1" ]`).

diff --git a/images/sandbox/Dockerfile b/images/sandbox/Dockerfile
index d2d55fd..deafb6f 100644
--- a/images/sandbox/Dockerfile
+++ b/images/sandbox/Dockerfile
@@ -11,19 +11,27 @@ RUN apt-get update && apt-get install -y \
     jq ripgrep fd-find htop wget unzip ca-certificates net-tools iputils-ping \
     && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*
 
-# GUI tools: TigerVNC (Xvnc = virtual X server + VNC server combined), and Google Chrome
+# GUI tools: TigerVNC (Xvnc = virtual X server + VNC server combined), and browser
 # Xvnc sends the RFB banner immediately on connect, unlike x11vnc 0.9.17+ which waits
 # for client data first (breaking websockify's server-speaks-first expectation).
 RUN apt-get update && apt-get install -y \
     tigervnc-standalone-server openbox xterm xfonts-base xfonts-100dpi xfonts-75dpi \
-    fonts-liberation libappindicator3-1 libasound2t64 libatk-bridge2.0-0t64 \
-    libatk1.0-0t64 libcups2t64 libdbus-1-3 libgdk-pixbuf-2.0-0 libnspr4 libnss3 \
-    libx11-xcb1 libxcomposite1 libxdamage1 libxrandr2 xdg-utils \
-    && wget -q https://dl.google.com/linux/direct/google-chrome-stable_current_amd64.deb \
-    && apt-get install -y ./google-chrome-stable_current_amd64.deb \
-    && rm google-chrome-stable_current_amd64.deb \
     && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*
 
+# Google Chrome (amd64) or Chromium (arm64) — Google doesn't ship a Chrome deb for arm64
+ARG TARGETARCH
+RUN if [ "$TARGETARCH" = "amd64" ]; then \
+      apt-get update \
+      && wget -q https://dl.google.com/linux/direct/google-chrome-stable_current_amd64.deb \
+      && apt-get install -y ./google-chrome-stable_current_amd64.deb \
+      && rm google-chrome-stable_current_amd64.deb \
+      && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*; \
+    else \
+      apt-get update \
+      && apt-get install -y chromium \
+      && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*; \
+    fi
+
 # Prefer IPv4 to avoid slow/broken IPv6 connections
 RUN sed -i 's/#precedence ::ffff:0:0\/96  100/precedence ::ffff:0:0\/96  100/' /etc/gai.conf
 
diff --git a/images/sandbox/entrypoint.sh b/images/sandbox/entrypoint.sh

--- a/images/sandbox/entrypoint.sh

@@ -80,13 +80,20 @@ fi
 # the RFB banner immediately on connect — required for websockify compatibility.
 # x11vnc 0.9.17+ waits for client data before sending the banner, deadlocking
 # with websockify which also waits for the server to speak first.
-if command -v Xvnc &>/dev/null; then
-    Xvnc :99 -rfbport 5900 -SecurityTypes None -AlwaysShared \
-        -geometry 1920x1080 -depth 24 \

     fi
 fi

> AGENT

Here's a summary of all uncommitted changes:

---

## VNC Support (main feature)

**New VNC columns & validation** (`app/models/sandbox.rb`, `db/migrate/…_add_vnc_options_to_sandboxes.rb`):  
Added `vnc_enabled`, `vnc_geometry`, `vnc_depth` with constants `VNC_GEOMETRIES` and `VNC_DEPTHS` and validation.

**VNC options on create form** (`app/views/sandboxes/new.html.erb`):  
New "VNC / Display" section with enable toggle, resolution dropdown, and color depth dropdown. The options section hides/shows via JS when the checkbox is toggled.

**Pass VNC settings to containers** (`app/services/sandbox_manager.rb`):  
`container_env` now passes `SANDCASTLE_VNC_ENABLED`, `SANDCASTLE_VNC_GEOMETRY`, `SANDCASTLE_VNC_DEPTH` env vars when creating sandbox containers.

**Entrypoint respects VNC settings** (`images/sandbox/entrypoint.sh`):  
Reads those env vars, starts Xvnc + Openbox as the sandbox user (not root), creates log files with correct ownership. VNC can be disabled via `SANDCASTLE_VNC_ENABLED=0`.

**Dockerfile** (`images/sandbox/Dockerfile`):  
Chrome install split by arch — Google Chrome on amd64, Chromium on arm64. Chrome dependency list cleaned up (moved to distro to resolve).

**Chrome wrapper updated** (`images/sandbox/startchrome.sh`):  
Detects whether to use `google-chrome` or `chromium` binary at runtime (supports both amd64 and arm64).

**API & CLI** (`api/sandboxes_controller.rb`, `vendor/sandcastle-cli/api/types.go`, `cmd/sandbox.go`):  
VNC fields added to API create/show responses and to the Go CLI with `--no-vnc`, `--vnc-geometry`, `--vnc-depth` flags.

---

## Bug Fixes (this session)

**Dashboard stuck in "Creating"** (`app/controllers/dashboard_controller.rb`, `config/routes.rb`, `app/policies/sandbox_policy.rb`, `app/views/dashboard/_sandbox.html.erb`, `app/javascript/controllers/sandbox_polling_controller.js` [new]):  
Race condition where Solid Cable broadcasts fired before WebSocket subscribed. Fix: new `GET /sandboxes/:id/card` endpoint + `sandbox-polling` Stimulus controller that polls every 3s for in-progress sandboxes as a fallback.

**VNC/Terminal Bad Gateway** (`app/services/vnc_manager.rb`, `app/services/terminal_manager.rb`):  
`active?` only checked Docker container state, not whether the HTTP server was actually ready. Fix: TCP health check on port 6080 (noVNC) / 3000 (WeTTY) before declaring "ready".

**Terminal idempotent path missing Traefik config** (`app/services/terminal_manager.rb`):  
When WeTTY container was already running, `write_traefik_config` was skipped. Fixed.

**Terminal URL wrong hostname** (`docker-compose.local.yml`):  
`SANDCASTLE_TERMINAL_URL` pointed to `sandcastle.local:8443` (unresolvable from browser). Changed to `localhost:8443`.

**Migrate container Docker socket error** (`config/initializers/container_sync.rb`):  
`ContainerSyncJob` ran at startup even in the migrate container (no Docker socket). Fixed with `!File.exist?("/var/run/docker.sock")` guard.

Perfect. Now I have all the information I need. Let me create a comprehensive analysis report:

## Comprehensive Production Deployment Assessment

Based on my thorough exploration of the Sandcastle codebase, here's a detailed assessment of uncommitted changes and their impact on production safety:

---

## SUMMARY: Safe to Merge to Production ✅

All uncommitted changes are **backend and CLI enhancements** for the VNC feature. The production deployment configuration (`docker-compose.yml`, `installer.sh`, `install-defaults`) is **unchanged**. No breaking changes detected.

---

## Production Deployment Configuration (No Changes)

### `docker-compose.yml` (production)
**Status:** ✅ Unchanged
- Uses `ghcr.io/thieso2/sandcastle:latest` (production image)
- No `SANDCASTLE_TERMINAL_URL` env var (derived from `SANDCASTLE_HOST`)
- No `SANDCASTLE_VNC_URL` env var exists anywhere
- Database passwords preserved via `/data/postgres/.secrets`
- Traefik configured at `/data/traefik/traefik.yml` and `/data/traefik/dynamic/`

### `installer.sh` (production installer)
**Status:** ✅ Unchanged
- Generates compose file dynamically with correct paths
- Handles BTRFS subvolume creation
- Sets up passwordless sudo restricted to BTRFS commands
- Database password preservation logic intact
- Traefik config generation unchanged

### `install-defaults` (configuration reference)
**Status:** ✅ Unchanged
- All default values intact
- No new required env vars introduced

---

## Key Production Env Vars

| Variable | Producer | Usage | Production Value |
|----------|----------|-------|---|
| `SANDCASTLE_HOST` | Installer config | Traefik routing rule, SSH connect command | User-provided (e.g., `sandcastle.example.com`) |
| `SANDCASTLE_TLS_MODE` | Installer config | Traefik cert resolver | `letsencrypt` or `selfsigned` |
| `SANDCASTLE_DATA_DIR` | Installer config | Container data mount | `/data` (bind-mounted on host) |
| `SECRET_KEY_BASE` | Installer (random) | Rails encryption | Random 128-char hex on fresh install |
| `DB_PASSWORD` | Installer (from `.secrets` file) | PostgreSQL auth | Persisted in `/data/postgres/.secrets` |
| `DOCKER_GID` | Installer (detected) | Docker socket group | Detected from host socket (usually 988) |
| `SANDCASTLE_TERMINAL_URL` | **NOT SET** | — | Uses `SANDCASTLE_HOST` by default |
| `SANDCASTLE_VNC_URL` | **DOES NOT EXIST** | — | — |

---

## Uncommitted Changes (All Safe for Production)

### 1. **Database Migration** (NEW)
**File:** `db/migrate/20260219100000_add_vnc_options_to_sandboxes.rb`

```ruby
add_column :sandboxes, :vnc_enabled, :boolean, default: true
add_column :sandboxes, :vnc_geometry, :string, default: "1280x900"
add_column :sandboxes, :vnc_depth, :integer, default: 24
```

**Impact:** ✅ **SAFE**
- Additive only (no deletions, no renames)
- Default values ensure backward compatibility
- All existing sandboxes get VNC enabled by default
- Migrations are run during deployment (`docker compose run migrate`)

### 2. **Backend Models & Controllers**

#### `app/models/sandbox.rb`
- Added VNC field validations
- `VNC_GEOMETRIES` and `VNC_DEPTHS` constants
- No breaking changes to existing validations

**Impact:** ✅ **SAFE** — Additive only

#### `app/controllers/api/sandboxes_controller.rb`
- Lines 32-34: VNC params in create request (API contract expansion)
- Defaults to `vnc_enabled: true` if not specified

**Impact:** ✅ **SAFE** — API is backward compatible (old clients don't send VNC fields, get defaults)

#### `app/controllers/sandboxes_controller.rb`
- Lines 28-30: VNC fields in web form create
- Same default behavior as API

**Impact:** ✅ **SAFE**

### 3. **Container Image Changes** (CRITICAL FOR DEPLOYMENT)

#### `images/sandbox/Dockerfile`
**Changes:** Lines 14-19 (TigerVNC + Openbox instead of Xvfb + x11vnc)

```dockerfile
# OLD:
# RUN apt-get install -y xvfb x11vnc xfonts-base ...

# NEW:
RUN apt-get install -y \
    tigervnc-standalone-server openbox xterm xfonts-base xfonts-100dpi xfonts-75dpi
```

**Why:** TigerVNC's Xvnc sends the RFB banner immediately on connect (required for websockify), whereas x11vnc 0.9.17+ waits for client data first.

**Impact:** ⚠️ **REQUIRES IMAGE REBUILD**
- Current production sandboxes use old image
- New sandboxes created with `:latest` tag will pull new image
- Existing sandboxes won't be affected (cached in container)
- **Action:** Build and push new image before production deployment:
  ```bash
  docker build -t ghcr.io/thieso2/sandcastle-sandbox:latest images/sandbox/
  docker push ghcr.io/thieso2/sandcastle-sandbox:latest
  ```

#### `images/sandbox/entrypoint.sh`
**Changes:** Lines 78-98 (VNC startup with TigerVNC/Xvnc)

```bash
# NEW:
if command -v Xvnc &>/dev/null && [ "$VNC_ENABLED" = "1" ]; then
    su -s /bin/bash "$USERNAME" -c \
        "Xvnc :99 -rfbport 5900 -SecurityTypes None -AlwaysShared ..."
    # Start Openbox window manager
    su -s /bin/bash "$USERNAME" -c \
        'DISPLAY=:99 openbox &>/var/log/openbox.log &'
fi
```

**New Env Vars:**
- `SANDCASTLE_VNC_ENABLED` (default: 1)
- `SANDCASTLE_VNC_GEOMETRY` (default: 1280x900)
- `SANDCASTLE_VNC_DEPTH` (default: 24)

**Impact:** ✅ **SAFE**
- Env vars have sensible defaults
- VNC disabled if `Xvnc` not found
- Openbox only started if available

### 4. **Service Layer Changes**

#### `app/services/sandbox_manager.rb`
- Lines 30 & 32: Validate `vnc_geometry` and `vnc_depth`
- Lines 60: Pass VNC env vars to container
- No breaking changes to existing methods

**Impact:** ✅ **SAFE**

#### `app/services/vnc_manager.rb`
**Key change:** `active?` method now checks TCP port 6080

```ruby
def active?(sandbox:)
  container_name = vnc_container_name(sandbox)
  return false unless container_running?(container_name)
  TCPSocket.new(container_name, 6080).close  # Check websockify is ready
  true
rescue Errno::ECONNREFUSED, Errno::ETIMEDOUT, SocketError
  false
end
```

**Why:** Docker reports container as "running" before websockify binds to port 6080.

**Impact:** ✅ **SAFE** — Better robustness

### 5. **CLI Changes** (Backward Compatible)

#### `vendor/sandcastle-cli/cmd/sandbox.go`
**New flags:**
- `--no-vnc` (disable VNC)
- `--vnc-geometry` (e.g., `1920x1080`)
- `--vnc-depth` (8, 16, 24, or 32)

**Impact:** ✅ **SAFE** — Flags are optional, old CLI invocations still work

#### `vendor/sandcastle-cli/api/types.go`
**New fields in `CreateSandboxRequest`:**
- `VNCEnabled bool`
- `VNCGeometry string`
- `VNCDepth int`

**Impact:** ✅ **SAFE** — API is backward compatible (old clients omit fields, get defaults)

### 6. **Web UI Form Changes**

#### `app/views/sandboxes/new.html.erb`
- Added VNC section (lines 97-140)
- JavaScript to toggle VNC options visibility
- Uses same form parameter structure as API

**Impact:** ✅ **SAFE** — UI-only, no backend breaking changes

### 7. **Development Config** (NOT PRODUCTION)

#### `docker-compose.local.yml`
- Line 113: `SANDCASTLE_TERMINAL_URL: https://localhost:8443`

**Note:** This is LOCAL ONLY, not used in production
- Production uses `SANDCASTLE_HOST` from env vars
- TerminalManager derives redirect URL from `SANDCASTLE_HOST`

**Impact:** ✅ **SAFE** — Local dev only

---

## Production Deployment Checklist

Before deploying to production:

- [ ] **1. Build & push new sandbox image**
  ```bash
  docker build -t ghcr.io/thieso2/sandcastle-sandbox:latest images/sandbox/
  docker push ghcr.io/thieso2/sandcastle-sandbox:latest
  ```

- [ ] **2. Ensure installer.sh is NOT edited**
  - All changes are in generated compose file (`docker-compose.yml`)
  - Installer flow unchanged

- [ ] **3. Migration will run automatically**
  - During `docker compose run migrate db:prepare`
  - Adds 3 columns with defaults

- [ ] **4. No new env vars required**
  - All VNC behavior is opt-in or defaults to safe values
  - Existing `.env` files work unchanged

- [ ] **5. No database password changes**
  - Reused from `/data/postgres/.secrets`
  - Existing production data preserved

- [ ] **6. Sandbox image rollout**
  - Old running sandboxes: unaffected (use cached image)
  - New sandboxes: pull latest image with VNC support
  - CLI `--image` flag can specify old image if needed

---

## Env Var Summary (No Production Changes)

### Production (`docker-compose.yml`)
```yaml
RAILS_ENV: production
SECRET_KEY_BASE: ${SECRET_KEY_BASE}           # From .env
SANDCASTLE_HOST: ${SANDCASTLE_HOST}           # From .env
SANDCASTLE_DATA_DIR: /data
SANDCASTLE_TLS_MODE: ${SANDCASTLE_TLS_MODE}   # From .env
DB_HOST: postgres
DB_USER: sandcastle
DB_PASSWORD: ${DB_PASSWORD}                   # From .env/.secrets
```

### New (VNC) Env Vars in Sandbox Container
```bash
SANDCASTLE_VNC_ENABLED: 1                     # Always set by SandboxManager
SANDCASTLE_VNC_GEOMETRY: 1280x900              # From sandbox.vnc_geometry
SANDCASTLE_VNC_DEPTH: 24                       # From sandbox.vnc_depth
```

These are set per-sandbox in `SandboxManager#container_env()`, not globally.

---

## Files Content Reference

### Key Files (All Read, No Changes to Deployment):
1. **`docker-compose.yml`** — Production compose (UNCHANGED)
2. **`installer.sh`** — Deployment script (UNCHANGED) 
3. **`install-defaults`** — Config defaults (UNCHANGED)
4. **`config/initializers/traefik.rb`** — Traefik init (minimal, writes Rails route config)

### Unchanged in Production:
- Traefik configuration logic
- SANDCASTLE_TERMINAL_URL derivation (uses SANDCASTLE_HOST)
- Database password preservation
- BTRFS subvolume handling
- Firewall rules (UFW)
- SSL/TLS mode handling (selfsigned vs letsencrypt)

---

## Conclusion

**All uncommitted changes are production-safe.** The VNC feature is:
- ✅ Backward compatible at API and CLI levels
- ✅ Additive only (no breaking changes)
- ✅ Opt-in with sensible defaults
- ✅ No new required env vars
- ✅ Database migration is additive

**Only action needed:** Build and push the new sandbox image with TigerVNC before production deployment so new sandboxes get VNC support.

installer/installer.sh.in:        -subj "/CN=$SANDCASTLE_HOST" \
installer/installer.sh.in:        -addext "subjectAltName=IP:$SANDCASTLE_HOST" 2>/dev/null
installer/installer.sh.in:  if [ "$SANDCASTLE_TLS_MODE" = "selfsigned" ]; then
installer/installer.sh.in:      rule: "Host(\`${SANDCASTLE_HOST}\`)"
installer/installer.sh.in:  local base_url="https://${SANDCASTLE_HOST}${port_suffix}"
installer/installer.sh.in:  if [ "$SANDCASTLE_TLS_MODE" = "selfsigned" ]; then

Good — both fall back gracefully to relative paths when the env var isn't set. That's the production behavior.

---

## Risk Assessment

**Short answer: nothing will break production.** But there are a few things worth knowing:

---

### ✅ Safe for production as-is

| Change | Why safe |
|--------|----------|
| DB migration (add vnc columns) | Additive, all columns have `NOT NULL` defaults — existing rows auto-fill |
| Model validations (`vnc_geometry`, `vnc_depth`) | Only validate new columns; existing rows already satisfy defaults |
| API create/show VNC fields | Old clients don't send them → defaults apply |
| `sandbox_manager.rb` VNC env vars | Passed into sandbox containers only; defaults baked into entrypoint.sh |
| `vnc_manager.rb` / `terminal_manager.rb` TCP health check | Strictly safer than before |
| Dashboard polling fallback (Stimulus controller) | Additive, no side effects |
| `container_sync.rb` Docker socket guard | Only adds protection for migrate container |
| `config/routes.rb` card route | Additive |
| Go CLI VNC flags | All optional with defaults |

---

### ⚠️ Local-only change — must NOT go to production as-is

**`docker-compose.local.yml`** — `SANDCASTLE_TERMINAL_URL: https://localhost:8443`

This file is **not** used in production (`docker-compose.yml` is), so the change is harmless. But if someone copied this value into production it would break terminal access. Worth noting the distinction clearly.

Production **never sets** `SANDCASTLE_TERMINAL_URL` — the controllers fall back to relative paths, which is correct because Traefik is the single HTTPS entry point:
```ruby
base = ENV["SANDCASTLE_TERMINAL_URL"]
base ? "#{base}#{path}" : path  # production: base is nil → relative path
```

---

### ⚠️ Sandbox image must be rebuilt before VNC works in production

The production sandbox image (`ghcr.io/thieso2/sandcastle-sandbox:latest`) on the registry predates these changes. The entrypoint.sh and Dockerfile changes only take effect when a new image is built and pushed.

**Risk if deployed without rebuilding:** New sandboxes created in production will use the old image → no Xvnc → VNC button opens, noVNC container starts, browser gets a "failed to connect" error from noVNC (not a 502 — noVNC itself loads fine, it just can't reach port 5900). Not catastrophic, but the feature won't work.

**No risk to existing functionality** — SSH/terminal/everything else is unaffected.

---

### Deployment order when you're ready

```bash
# 1. Build and push new sandbox image (takes ~15 min)
docker build --platform linux/amd64 -t ghcr.io/thieso2/sandcastle-sandbox:latest images/sandbox/
docker push ghcr.io/thieso2/sandcastle-sandbox:latest

# 2. Deploy Rails app normally (migration runs automatically)
mise run release
# or ssh + docker compose pull && docker compose up -d
```
